    49	  }

+// ===========================================================================
+// ITEM 9 (v1.1) — pipeline smoke gate. After the FINAL subtask merges, a
+// pipeline whose loop.json sets smoke.cmd reuses the loop's smoke machinery
+// (smokeApprovalToken/verifySmokeToken, SMOKE_AWAIT/SMOKE_APPROVE, --approve-smoke):
+// run smoke.cmd, await a drift-bound token, complete only on a verified token.
+describe('pipeline — smoke gate (item 9)', () => {
+  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
+  // A git that also answers the smoke command (non-git → 'ok'), so merges work
+  // AND the smoke.cmd runs through io.execFile. Records every non-git (smoke)
+  // exec with a snapshot of the merge receipts AT that moment, so a test can
+  // prove the smoke command ran ONLY after the final subtask's merge.
+  const smokeAwareIo = (spec, { git = pipelineGit() } = {}) => {
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec, git, runner: undefined });
+    const baseExec = io.execFile;
+    const smokeRuns = /** @type {Array<{cmd: string, mergesAtRun: string}>} */ ([]);
+    io.execFile = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
+      if (cmd === 'git') return baseExec(cmd, args, opts);
+      smokeRuns.push({ cmd, mergesAtRun: io.files()[`${DIR}/merges.ndjson`] ?? '' });
+      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
+    };
+    io.__smokeRuns = smokeRuns;
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]); // two subtasks
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    return io;
+  };
+  const smokeSpec = () => pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }], smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' } });
+
+  it('RED (9-1): smoke.cmd RUNS only after the FINAL subtask merges, then the run AWAITS a genuine token', async () => {
+    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `awaiting smoke approval is a clean exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run awaits smoke approval, not DONE');
+    // The smoke command ran EXACTLY once, and only after BOTH subtasks merged.
+    assert.equal(io.__smokeRuns.length, 1, 'smoke.cmd ran exactly once');
+    assert.match(io.__smokeRuns[0].mergesAtRun, /"subtaskId":"t1"/, 'smoke ran after t1 merged');
+    assert.match(io.__smokeRuns[0].mergesAtRun, /"subtaskId":"t2"/, 'smoke ran only after the FINAL subtask (t2) merged');
+    assert.ok(io.files()[`${DIR}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`], 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
+    // The issued token is a genuine smokeApprovalToken over the recorded inputs.
+    const approval = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`]);
+    assert.ok(approval && typeof approval.token === 'string' && approval.token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'a smk token is issued');
+    assert.equal(approval.token, smokeApprovalToken(approval.inputs), 'the token is a genuine smokeApprovalToken over the recorded inputs');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (9-2): pipeline run --approve-smoke <token> verifies and completes the run DONE', async () => {
+    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
+    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
+    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
+    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 reached the smoke gate and issued a token');
+    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
+
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `an approved pipeline completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    const raw = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
+    assert.equal(raw.status, 'done', 'the approved run persists status done');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (9-2b): a BOGUS token with NO drift is still refused — the run does NOT complete', async () => {
+    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
+    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
+    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
+    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 issued a token');
+    // No drift — the ONLY defect is a wrong token value.
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', 'smk1.BOGUS-NOT-THE-ISSUED-TOKEN'], io);
+    assert.notEqual(code, 0, 'a bogus token is refused (not a clean completion)');
+    assert.notEqual(JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]).status, 'done', 'a bogus token never marks the run done');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (9-3): a DRIFTED smoke token is refused and the pipeline PARKS (exit 4)', async () => {
+    const git = pipelineGit();
+    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec(), { git });
+    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
+    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
+    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 issued a token');
+    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
+    // A bound input drifts: main moves after the human looked.
+    git.moveMain('DRIFTED-SHA-9-3-AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA');
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a drifted token parks the pipeline (exit 4)');
+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run');
+  });
+
+  it('GUARD (9-4): a pipeline with NO smoke.cmd completes straight to DONE (no regression)', async () => {
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0);
+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'no smoke.cmd → straight to DONE');
+    assert.ok(!io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`], 'no smoke gate is armed when smoke.cmd is unset');
+  });
+
+  it('GUARD (9-5): a pipeline spec with smoke.cmd and NO phases is ACCEPTED — the loop smoke-build rule never gates pipelines', async () => {
+    const spec = smokeSpec();
+    assert.ok(!('phases' in spec), 'precondition: the pipeline spec has subtasks, no phases');
+    const io = smokeAwareIo(spec);
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'smoke.cmd + no phases is NOT a validation error for a pipeline (no smoke-build refusal)');
+    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'it ran past validation and spawned the subtask');
+  });
+});

+// ===========================================================================
+// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach (pipeline half). Mirrors the loop detach contract
+// through the same injected `io.spawnDetached` seam (see loop-run.test.mjs for
+// the full seam rationale). POSIX-only — SKIP_WIN.
+describe('pipeline — --detach (item 10)', () => {
+  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
+  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
+  const basePipe = () => {
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    return io;
+  };
+  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
+    const rec = { calls: [] };
+    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
+      rec.calls.push(spec);
+      if (writeLock) {
+        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
+      }
+      return { pid };
+    };
+    return rec;
+  };
+
+  it('RED (10-1 pipeline): pipeline run --detach forks a detached supervisor; parent exits 0, lock names the detached pid, stdout prints pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = basePipe();
+    const DETACHED_PID = 515151;
+    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
+    assert.equal(JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]).pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
+    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
+    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (10-2 pipeline): pipeline run --detach refuses (exit 1) under a LIVE lock — no fork (parity via the shared lock path)', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = basePipe();
+    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 55555;
+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 1);
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'pipe-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'live' }));
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live lock refuses the detached pipeline run (exit 1)');
+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 0, 'no detached supervisor is forked while the lock is held live');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (10-3 pipeline): pipeline run --detach reclaims a DEAD lock, killing recorded child groups BEFORE forking', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = basePipe();
+    const events = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
+    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { events.push({ kind: 'kill', pid, sig }); return true; };
+    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 9001;
+    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
+      events.push({ kind: 'fork', spec });
+      io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: 7, startTime: 1, runId: 'sup-7' }));
+      return { pid: 7 };
+    };
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'pipe-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-t1-writer', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming detached run starts; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    const killIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'kill' && e.pid === -9001);
+    const forkIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'fork');
+    assert.ok(killIdx >= 0 && forkIdx >= 0 && killIdx < forkIdx, `the orphan kill (-9001) precedes the fork; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
+  });
+
+  it('RED (10-5 pipeline): pipeline run --detach directs output to a byte-capped .handoff/loop/supervisor.out', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = basePipe();
+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
+    await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the detach seam was invoked');
+    const spec = seam.calls[0];
+    assert.match(String(spec.outPath ?? ''), /\.handoff\/loop\/supervisor\.out$/, 'spec.outPath directs output to supervisor.out');
+    assert.ok(typeof spec.maxBytes === 'number' && spec.maxBytes > 0, `spec.maxBytes is a positive byte cap; got ${JSON.stringify(spec.maxBytes)}`);
+  });
+});

+// ===========================================================================
+// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach. `baton loop run --detach` forks a background
+// supervisor that survives the invoking terminal; the parent returns promptly
+// once the detached child owns the run lock, printing its pid + a tail hint.
+//
+// SEAM JUDGMENT CALL (flagged for the verifier): a REAL fork is not honestly
+// unit-testable (an orphaned process, timing), so these pin the behavior through
+// an injected `io.spawnDetached(spec) -> { pid }` seam the implementation should
+// honor (default: a real detached child_process.spawn). Assumptions pinned:
+//   - the PARENT checks the run lock (refuse-live / reclaim-dead) BEFORE forking;
+//   - the DETACHED CHILD owns the lock (the fake writes it naming the child pid);
+//   - spawnDetached receives the capped supervisor.out output path.
+// Windows: --detach is POSIX-only (documented unsupported) — SKIP_WIN.
+describe('loop run — --detach (item 10)', () => {
+  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
+  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
+  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
+    const rec = { calls: [] };
+    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
+      rec.calls.push(spec);
+      if (writeLock) {
+        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
+      }
+      return { pid };
+    };
+    return rec;
+  };
+
+  it('RED (10-1): --detach forks a detached supervisor; the parent exits 0 after the child owns the lock, printing pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
+    const DETACHED_PID = 424242;
+    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
+    const lock = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]);
+    assert.equal(lock.pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
+    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
+    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (10-2): --detach refuses (exit 1) when a LIVE supervisor already holds the lock — no fork', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
+    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 55555;
+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 1);
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'live' }));
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live lock refuses the detached run (exit 1)');
+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 0, 'no detached supervisor is forked while the lock is held live');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (10-3): --detach reclaims a DEAD lock, killing recorded in-flight child groups BEFORE forking', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
+    // ONE shared ordered event log — the -9001 kill must precede the fork.
+    const events = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
+    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { events.push({ kind: 'kill', pid, sig }); return true; };
+    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 9001; // orphan group alive; lock pid 999999 dead
+    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
+      events.push({ kind: 'fork', spec });
+      io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: 7, startTime: 1, runId: 'sup-7' }));
+      return { pid: 7 };
+    };
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming detached run starts; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    const killIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'kill' && e.pid === -9001);
+    const forkIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'fork');
+    assert.ok(killIdx >= 0, `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
+    assert.ok(forkIdx >= 0, 'the detached supervisor was forked after reclaiming');
+    assert.ok(killIdx < forkIdx, `the orphan kill (-9001) precedes the fork; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
+  });
+
+  it('GUARD (10-4): a completing run releases the lock — the release contract a detached supervisor reuses', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    // The detached child runs the same supervisor body; isolating its release
+    // needs a real child, so this pins the reused contract on the attached path.
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] }), runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'a completing run releases the supervisor.lock');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (10-5): --detach directs the detached output to a byte-capped .handoff/loop/supervisor.out', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
+    await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the detach seam was invoked');
+    const spec = seam.calls[0];
+    assert.match(String(spec.outPath ?? ''), /\.handoff\/loop\/supervisor\.out$/, 'spec.outPath directs the detached output to .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
+    assert.ok(typeof spec.maxBytes === 'number' && spec.maxBytes > 0, `spec.maxBytes is a positive byte cap for supervisor.out; got ${JSON.stringify(spec.maxBytes)}`);
+  });
+});
+
 // ===========================================================================

   137	    84	// A seeded handoff bundle so a pipeline writer limit-death has a real bundle to
   138	    85	// seal/receive through runFailover.
   139	    86	function ownedBundle(originPlatform = 'claude-code', status = 'open') {
   140	    87	  return {
   141	    88	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
   142	    89	    bundleId: 'b_pipe00000000000',
   143	    90	    generation: 1,
   144	    91	    createdAt: T0,
   145	    92	    updatedAt: T0,
   146	    93	    origin: { platform: originPlatform, model: 'm', sessionHint: 'loop-sup', unstable: false },

   154	   101	      status === 'sealed'
   155	   102	        ? { status: 'sealed', reason: 'seal', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: T0, receive_log: [] }
   156	   103	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   157	   104	    journalSeq: 0,

   163	   110	/**
   164	   111	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
   165	   112	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
   166	   113	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
   167	   114	 */
   168	   115	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG, existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], mergedBranches = [] } = {}) {
   169	   116	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
   170	   117	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
   171	   118	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches); // stale/pre-existing namespaced branches
   172	   119	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds); // seat cwds whose index is dirty
   173	   120	  const deleted = new Set(); // seats listed by git but raw-deleted on disk
   174	   121	  const merged = new Set(mergedBranches); // branches already merged into main (ancestor; empty main..branch)
   175	   122	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   176	   123	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
   177	   124	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
   178	   125	
   179	   126	  const list = () => {
   180	   127	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
   181	   128	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
   182	   129	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   183	   130	  };
   184	   131	
   185	   132	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   186	   133	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   187	   134	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   188	   135	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   189	   136	
   190	   137	    // Explicit overrides win.
   191	   138	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   192	   139	    if (okey) {
   193	   140	      const r = over[okey];
   194	   141	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   195	   142	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   196	   143	    }
   197	   144	
   198	   145	    // A raw-deleted seat: git still LISTS it, but any command IN that cwd fails
   199	   146	    // (the directory is gone). Only self-heal (prune + re-add) recovers it.
   200	   147	    if (opts?.cwd && deleted.has(opts.cwd) && !argstr.startsWith('worktree ')) {
   201	   148	      return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: cannot chdir to '${opts.cwd}': No such file or directory`), { code: 128, stderr: 'No such file or directory\n' }));
   202	   149	    }
   203	   150	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   204	   151	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   205	   152	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   206	   153	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   207	   154	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   208	   155	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); deleted.delete(path); }
   209	   156	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   210	   157	    }
   211	   158	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune')) { deleted.clear(); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   212	   159	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   213	   160	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
   214	   161	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
   215	   162	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
   216	   163	      branches.add(target);
   217	   164	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
   218	   165	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   219	   166	    }
   220	   167	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
   221	   168	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   222	   169	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   223	   170	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> main : exit 0 iff <branch> is already in main.
   224	   171	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
   225	   172	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
   226	   173	      return merged.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
   227	   174	    }
   228	   175	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
   229	   176	      // A merged branch has NO commits ahead of main (empty main..<branch>).
   230	   177	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
   231	   178	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
   232	   179	      if (branch && merged.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   233	   180	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   234	   181	    }
   235	   182	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   236	   183	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   237	   184	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   238	   185	  };
   239	   186	  fn.calls = calls;
   240	   187	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   241	   188	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   242	   189	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   243	   190	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   244	   191	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   245	   192	  fn.moveMain = (/** @type {string} */ sha) => { mainSha = sha; }; // a child "moves main" mid-subtask
   246	   193	  fn.setBranch = (/** @type {string} */ cwd, /** @type {string} */ branch) => { branchAt.set(cwd, branch); };
   247	   194	  // Explicit seed: git LISTS this worktree; `raw` marks its dir as deleted on
   248	   195	  // disk (any in-cwd command fails until prune + re-add self-heals it).
   249	   196	  fn.seedWorktree = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ branch, /** @type {{raw?: boolean}} */ o = {}) => {
   250	   197	    added.set(path, branch);
   251	   198	    branchAt.set(path, branch);
   252	   199	    if (o.raw) deleted.add(path);
   253	   200	  };
   254	   201	  return fn;
   255	   202	}
   256	   203	
   257	   204	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   258	   205	  const queue = [...script];
   259	   206	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   260	   207	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   261	   208	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   262	   209	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   263	   210	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   264	   211	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   265	   212	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   266	   213	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   267	   214	    }
   268	   215	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   269	   216	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   270	   217	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   271	   218	  };
   272	   219	  fn.calls = calls;
   273	   220	  return fn;
   274	   221	}
   275	   222	
   276	   223	function makePipeRepo({ spec = pipelineSpec(), config = PIPELINE_CONFIG, files = {}, git, runner, env = {} } = {}) {
   277	   224	  const io = makeIo({
   278	   225	    now: T0,
   279	   226	    host: 'pipe-host',
   280	   227	    pid: 4242,
   281	   228	    startTime: 111000,
   282	   229	    env,
   283	   230	    files: {
   284	   231	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
   285	   232	      '/repo/.git/HEAD': 'ref: refs/heads/main\n',
   286	   233	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(config, null, 2),
   287	   234	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
   288	   235	      ...files,
   289	   236	    },
   290	   237	  });
   291	   238	  const g = git ?? pipelineGit();
   292	   239	  g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK));
   293	   240	  io.execFile = g;
   294	   241	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
   295	   242	  io.superviseChild = fake;
   296	   243	  io.__git = g;
   297	   244	  io.__runner = fake;
   298	   245	  return io;
   299	   246	}
   300	   247	
   301	   248	// A full clean subtask = writer APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED.
   302	   249	const cleanSubtask = () => [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }];
   303	   250	const argsOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String);
   304	   251	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
   305	   252	const childWithModel = (runner, model) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).includes(model));
   306	   253	// Writers = the write-capable children (in call order). Reviewers = read-only
   307	   254	// children whose prompt carries the 'subtask-reviewer' role identity (so the
   308	   255	// merger, also read-only, is excluded).
   309	   256	const writersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   310	   257	const reviewersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only' && argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('subtask-reviewer'));
   311	   258	
   312	   259	// ===========================================================================
   313	   260	describe('pipeline — CLI routing & flags', () => {
   314	   261	  it('no subcommand -> usage error (exit 2) naming a subcommand, not "unknown command"', async () => {
   315	   262	    const io = makePipeRepo();
   316	   263	    const code = await run(['pipeline'], io);
   317	   264	    assert.equal(code, 2);
   318	   265	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   319	   266	    assert.match(out, /subcommand|run/i, 'the error guides toward a subcommand');
   320	   267	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /unknown command/i, 'pipeline IS a known command');
   321	   268	  });
   322	   269	
   323	   270	  it('an unknown subcommand exits 2 naming it', async () => {
   324	   271	    const io = makePipeRepo();
   325	   272	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'frobnicate'], io);
   326	   273	    assert.equal(code, 2);
   327	   274	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /frobnicate/, 'the error names the bad subcommand');
   328	   275	  });
   329	   276	
   330	   277	  it('a stray positional / unknown flag exits 2 with zero spawns', async () => {
   331	   278	    const io1 = makePipeRepo();
   332	   279	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run', 'stray'], io1), 2);
   333	   280	    assert.equal(io1.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'a garbled invocation spawns nothing');
   334	   281	    const io2 = makePipeRepo();
   335	   282	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--no-such-flag'], io2), 2);
   336	   283	    assert.equal(io2.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'an unknown flag spawns nothing');
   337	   284	  });
   338	   285	
   339	   286	  it('BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD does NOT suppress pipeline run (supervisor-side)', async () => {
   340	   287	    const io = makePipeRepo({ env: { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1' }, runner: undefined });
   341	   288	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   342	   289	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   343	   290	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   344	   291	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the guard is child-only; the supervisor command still runs');
   345	   292	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'children spawned under the guard env');
   346	   293	  });
   347	   294	});
   348	   295	
   349	   296	// ===========================================================================
   350	   297	describe('pipeline — preconditions', () => {
   351	   298	  it('no loop.json -> exit 2 naming loop.json, zero spawns', async () => {
   352	   299	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: null });
   353	   300	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   354	   301	    assert.equal(code, 2);
   355	   302	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
   356	   303	    assert.match(out, /loop\.json/i, 'the error names the missing spec file');
   357	   304	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /unknown command/i, 'pipeline is routed — the error is about the missing spec, not the command');
   358	   305	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned');
   359	   306	  });
   360	   307	
   361	   308	  it('missing / empty subtasks -> exit 2 naming subtasks, zero spawns', async () => {
   362	   309	    for (const spec of [pipelineSpec({ subtasks: undefined }), pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [] })]) {
   363	   310	      const io = makePipeRepo({ spec });
   364	   311	      const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   365	   312	      assert.equal(code, 2, 'a pipeline needs subtasks');
   366	   313	      assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /subtasks/i, 'the error names subtasks');
   367	   314	      assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned without subtasks');
   368	   315	    }
   369	   316	  });
   370	   317	
   371	   318	  it('malformed subtask items exit 2 naming the offender, with zero spawns', async () => {
   372	   319	    const CASES = [
   373	   320	      ['missing id', [{ title: 'no id' }], /id/i],
   374	   321	      ['missing title', [{ id: 't1' }], /title/i],
   375	   322	      ['non-string id', [{ id: 7, title: 'ok' }], /id/i],
   376	   323	      ['non-string title', [{ id: 't1', title: 5 }], /title/i],
   377	   324	      ['duplicate ids', [{ id: 't1', title: 'a' }, { id: 't1', title: 'b' }], /duplicate|t1/i],
   378	   325	    ];
   379	   326	    for (const [label, subtasks, needle] of CASES) {
   380	   327	      const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }) });
   381	   328	      const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   382	   329	      assert.equal(code, 2, `${label} is a usage error`);
   383	   330	      assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), needle, `${label}: the error names the offender`);
   384	   331	      assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, `${label}: zero spawns`);
   385	   332	    }
   386	   333	  });
   387	   334	
   388	   335	  it('setupWorktrees runs before subtask 1 (worktree adds recorded) and only once', async () => {
   389	   336	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   390	   337	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   391	   338	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   392	   339	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   393	   340	    const adds = io.__git.matching(/^worktree add/);
   394	   341	    assert.equal(adds.length, 2, 'both seat worktrees are added exactly once, before the subtask cycle');
   395	   342	    assert.ok(adds.some((c) => c.argstr.includes(WT_A)) && adds.some((c) => c.argstr.includes(WT_B)), 'wt-a and wt-b are set up');
   396	   343	  });
   397	   344	});
   398	   345	
   399	   346	// ===========================================================================
   400	   347	describe('pipeline — seat alternation (writer)', () => {
   401	   348	  it('subtask 1 writes in wt-a on baton/wt-a/subtask-t1 (worker-a model); subtask 2 writes in wt-b on baton/wt-b/subtask-t2 (worker-b model)', async () => {
   402	   349	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   403	   350	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   404	   351	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   405	   352	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   406	   353	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   407	   354	
   408	   355	    // Branch creation recorded in each seat's own cwd.
   409	   356	    assert.equal(io.__git.withCwd(/^checkout -b baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1$/, WT_A).length, 1, 'subtask 1 branch created in wt-a');
   410	   357	    assert.equal(io.__git.withCwd(/^checkout -b baton\/wt-b\/subtask-t2$/, WT_B).length, 1, 'subtask 2 branch created in wt-b');
   411	   358	
   412	   359	    // The WRITERS are exactly the two write-capable children, in subtask order —
   413	   360	    // isolating by -s workspace-write means a read-only reviewer can never
   414	   361	    // satisfy this (finding 1).
   415	   362	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
   416	   363	    assert.equal(writers.length, 2, 'exactly two writer children (one per subtask)');
   417	   364	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '--model'), 'wa-model', 'subtask 1 writer uses the worker-a model');
   418	   365	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'subtask 1 writer works in wt-a');
   419	   366	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[1]), '--model'), 'wb-model', 'subtask 2 writer uses the worker-b model');
   420	   367	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[1]), '-C'), WT_B, 'subtask 2 writer works in wt-b');
   421	   368	  });
   422	   369	});
   423	   370	
   424	   371	// ===========================================================================
   425	   372	describe('pipeline — reviewer seat (read-only, other seat)', () => {
   426	   373	  it("both reviewers, in order: t1 = wb-model/WT_B/read-only, t2 = wa-model/WT_A/read-only, each under the subtask-reviewer role", async () => {
   427	   374	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   428	   375	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   429	   376	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   430	   377	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   431	   378	
   432	   379	    const reviewers = reviewersOf(io.__runner);
   433	   380	    assert.equal(reviewers.length, 2, 'exactly two reviewer children (one per subtask), provable via the subtask-reviewer role identity in the prompt');
   434	   381	    // t1 reviewer: OTHER seat is b -> wb-model in wt-b.
   435	   382	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[0]), '--model'), 'wb-model', 'subtask 1 reviewer carries the OTHER seat (worker-b) model');
   436	   383	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'subtask 1 reviewer reviews from wt-b');
   437	   384	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[0]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'subtask 1 reviewer cannot write');
   438	   385	    // t2 reviewer: OTHER seat is a -> wa-model in wt-a.
   439	   386	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[1]), '--model'), 'wa-model', 'subtask 2 reviewer carries the OTHER seat (worker-a) model');
   440	   387	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[1]), '-C'), WT_A, 'subtask 2 reviewer reviews from wt-a');
   441	   388	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(reviewers[1]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'subtask 2 reviewer cannot write');
   442	   389	  });
   443	   390	
   444	   391	  it('RED (G7): the reviewer prompt instructs the git RANGE review (git diff main..baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-<id>)', async () => {
   445	   392	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   446	   393	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   447	   394	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   448	   395	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   449	   396	    const rev = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   450	   397	    assert.ok(rev, 'a reviewer child ran');
   451	   398	    const prompt = argsOf(rev).join(' ');
   452	   399	    // Refs are shared across worktrees, so the reviewer must be told to inspect
   453	   400	    // the exact range with git (not left to guess "the diff").
   454	   401	    assert.match(prompt, /git diff main\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the reviewer prompt names the exact `git diff main..<branch>` range to review');
   455	   402	  });
   456	   403	});
   457	   404	
   458	   405	// ===========================================================================
   459	   406	describe('pipeline — verdict flow', () => {
   460	   407	  it('reviewer APPROVED -> the MERGER child runs (merger model); on its APPROVED the SUPERVISOR merges via mergeSubtask', async () => {
   461	   408	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   462	   409	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   463	   410	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   464	   411	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   465	   412	
   466	   413	    // The merger child ran on the merger chain model and is NON-git-capable
   467	   414	    // (read-only) — it can re-check but never merge itself (finding 4).
   468	   415	    const mergers = childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model');
   469	   416	    assert.ok(mergers.length >= 1, 'the merger role child runs after APPROVED');
   470	   417	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(mergers[0]), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the merger child is read-only — it cannot write/merge itself');
   471	   418	
   472	   419	    // The REAL merge happened through mergeSubtask (the supervisor). EVERY merge
   473	   420	    // and ff-only call carries the supervisor shape: merge at /repo, ff-only in a
   474	   421	    // seat cwd, all under the acquired merge lock.
   475	   422	    const m1 = io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1$/);
   476	   423	    assert.equal(m1.length, 1, 'the subtask-1 branch is merged exactly once');
   477	   424	    for (const c of io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//)) {
   478	   425	      assert.equal(c.cwd, '/repo', 'every subtask merge runs at the repo root');
   479	   426	      assert.equal(c.lockPresent, true, 'every subtask merge runs under the merge lock');
   480	   427	    }
   481	   428	    const ff = io.__git.matching(/^merge --ff-only main$/);
   482	   429	    assert.ok(ff.length >= 2, 'both worktrees fast-forward to main after each merge');
   483	   430	    for (const c of ff) {
   484	   431	      assert.ok(c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B, 'every ff-only sync runs in a seat worktree cwd');
   485	   432	      assert.equal(c.lockPresent, true, 'every ff-only sync runs under the merge lock');
   486	   433	    }
   487	   434	  });
   488	   435	
   489	   436	  it('reviewer BLOCKED -> the SAME writer seat re-runs with the findings in its prompt', async () => {
   490	   437	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   491	   438	    // subtask 1: writer APPROVED, reviewer BLOCKED (findings), then writer re-run APPROVED, reviewer APPROVED, merger APPROVED; then subtask 2 clean.
   492	   439	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   493	   440	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1
   494	   441	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'REVIEW-FINDING-X' }, // reviewer 1
   495	   442	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1 re-run
   496	   443	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer 1 again
   497	   444	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger 1
   498	   445	      ...cleanSubtask(), // subtask 2
   499	   446	    ]);
   500	   447	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   501	   448	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   502	   449	    // The re-run writer (2nd wa-model writer in wt-a) carries the findings.
   503	   450	    const writers = childWithModel(io.__runner, 'wa-model').filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write');
   504	   451	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 2, 'the writer seat re-ran after the BLOCKED review');
   505	   452	    assert.match(argsOf(writers[1]).join(' '), /REVIEW-FINDING-X/, "the re-run writer's prompt carries the review findings");
   506	   453	  });
   507	   454	
   508	   455	  it('review-gate cap exhaustion -> escalated, ESCALATION.md, exit 3, no 6th spawn', async () => {
   509	   456	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   510	   457	    // Reviewer BLOCKS five times; the 6th writer/reviewer pair must never spawn.
   511	   458	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   512	   459	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f1' },
   513	   460	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f2' },
   514	   461	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f3' },
   515	   462	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f4' },
   516	   463	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f5' },
   517	   464	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f6' }, // must NEVER be consumed
   518	   465	    ]);
   519	   466	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   520	   467	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   521	   468	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a per-subtask review gate at the cap escalates');
   522	   469	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md'), 'an ESCALATION.md is written');
   523	   470	    // Exactly five writer+reviewer cycles ran — the 6th pair (and the f6 result)
   524	   471	    // are never consumed, and the merger never runs (finding 3).
   525	   472	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 10, 'exactly 5 writers + 5 reviewers before escalation — no 6th spawn');
   526	   473	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 5, 'five writer attempts');
   527	   474	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 5, 'five review attempts');
   528	   475	    assert.equal(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model').length, 0, 'the merger never runs for an escalated subtask');
   529	   476	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   530	   477	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the escalation is persisted to loop state');
   531	   478	  });
   532	   479	
   533	   480	  it('merger BLOCKED -> the writer retries (same gate-cap discipline)', async () => {
   534	   481	    const io = makePipeRepo({ runner: undefined });
   535	   482	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   536	   483	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer 1
   537	   484	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer 1
   538	   485	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'MERGER-FINDING-Y' }, // merger 1 rejects
   539	   486	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer re-run
   540	   487	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   541	   488	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger approves
   542	   489	      ...cleanSubtask(),
   543	   490	    ]);
   544	   491	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   545	   492	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   546	   493	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the subtask recovers after a merger rejection + writer retry');
   547	   494	    assert.ok(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'wa-model').filter((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write').length >= 2, 'the writer retried after the merger BLOCKED');
   548	   495	  });
   549	   496	});
   550	   497	
   551	   498	// ===========================================================================
   552	   499	describe('pipeline — merge failures park', () => {
   553	   500	  it('a merge CONFLICT from mergeSubtask parks the run (exit 4)', async () => {
   554	   501	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'merge baton/wt-a/subtask-t1': { reject: true, code: 1, stdout: 'CONFLICT (content): Merge conflict in x' }, 'merge --abort': { stdout: '' } } });
   555	   502	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   556	   503	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());

   559	   506	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a merge conflict parks the pipeline');

   562	   509	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   563	   510	  });
   564	   511	
   565	   512	  it('an attribution violation (foreign committer) blocks the merge and parks — no merge git call lands', async () => {
   566	   513	    const bad = logStdout(REC('badc0de', NAME, EMAIL, 'Rebase Bot', 'bot@ci.example', 'foreign committer'));
   567	   514	    const git = pipelineGit({ log: bad });

   575	   522	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   576	   523	  });
   577	   524	});
   578	   525	
   579	   526	// ===========================================================================
   580	   527	describe('pipeline — worktree guard composition (preflight/postflight)', () => {
   581	   528	  it('(a) a DIRTY writer seat -> preflight refusal BEFORE any spawn: zero children, parked, exit 4', async () => {
   582	   529	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'status --porcelain': { stdout: ' M src/x.js\n' } } });
   583	   530	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   584	   531	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   585	   532	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   586	   533	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   587	   534	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a dirty seat parks the pipeline');
   588	   535	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'preflight refuses BEFORE any child spawns');
   589	   536	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a preflight refusal');
   590	   537	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   591	   538	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the parked state is persisted');
   592	   539	  });
   593	   540	
   594	   541	  it('(b) a WRONG-branch writer seat -> preflight refusal BEFORE any spawn: zero children, parked, exit 4', async () => {
   595	   542	    const git = pipelineGit({ over: { 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD': { stdout: 'some-other-branch\n' } } });
   596	   543	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   597	   544	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   598	   545	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   599	   546	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   600	   547	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a wrong-branch seat parks the pipeline');
   601	   548	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'preflight refuses BEFORE any child spawns');
   602	   549	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a preflight refusal');
   603	   550	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   604	   551	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   605	   552	  });
   606	   553	
   607	   554	  it('(c) POSTFLIGHT drift (the writer child moves main) -> parked, exit 4, no reviewer/merger, no merge', async () => {
   608	   555	    const git = pipelineGit();
   609	   556	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   610	   557	    // The writer child (first spawn) moves main between preflight-capture and postflight.
   611	   558	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   612	   559	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', effect: () => git.moveMain('bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb') },
   613	   560	      ...cleanSubtask(),
   614	   561	    ]);
   615	   562	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   616	   563	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   617	   564	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'postflight drift parks the pipeline');
   618	   565	    // Exactly one child ran (the writer); postflight refuses before the reviewer.
   619	   566	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'only the writer ran — no reviewer/merger after postflight refusal');
   620	   567	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 0, 'no reviewer spawned');
   621	   568	    assert.equal(childWithModel(io.__runner, 'mg-model').length, 0, 'no merger spawned');
   622	   569	    assert.ok(!io.__git.issued(/^merge baton\//), 'no merge on a postflight refusal');
   623	   570	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   624	   571	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   625	   572	  });
   626	   573	});

   629	   576	describe('pipeline — completion', () => {

   645	   592	// ===========================================================================
   646	   593	// G1 (A1/B1/B2) — a claude worker seat's writer child carries its model + cwd.
   647	   594	describe('pipeline — G1: a claude-code worker seat writer carries model + seat cwd', () => {
   648	   595	  // worker-b on claude-code so subtask 2's writer is a claude child.
   649	   596	  const CLAUDE_SEAT_CONFIG = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-b': ['claude-code/cc-writer'] } };
   650	   597
