   663	   610	
   664	   611	// ===========================================================================
   665	   612	// G2 (A2/B3) — pipeline preconditions, caps, resume, run-lock.
   666	   613	describe('pipeline — G2: cap validation / resume / run-lock / stale branch', () => {
   667	   614	  it('RED (G2): iterationCap 6 in loop.json is REJECTED (exit 2) BEFORE any spawn (the 5-cap is a hard invariant)', async () => {
   668	   615	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ budgets: { iterationCap: 6, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }) });
   669	   616	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   670	   617	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a spec asking for a 6th iteration is invalid');
   671	   618	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /iterationCap|cap/i, 'the error names the cap');
   672	   619	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawns for an invalid spec');
   673	   620	  });
   674	   621	
   675	   622	  it('RED (G2): a LIVE supervisor.lock refuses pipeline run (exit 1), zero spawns', async () => {
   676	   623	    const io = makePipeRepo();
   677	   624	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555;
   678	   625	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   679	   626	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   680	   627	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'pipe-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'other' }));
   681	   628	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   682	   629	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live supervisor blocks a concurrent pipeline run');
   683	   630	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /55555|lock|supervisor/i, 'the refusal names the live supervisor');
   684	   631	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned while another supervisor is live');
   685	   632	  });
   686	   633	
   687	   634	  it('RED (G2): a stale pre-existing subtask branch PARKS (exit 4) instead of crashing', async () => {
   688	   635	    const git = pipelineGit({ existingBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
   689	   636	    const io = makePipeRepo({ git, runner: undefined });
   690	   637	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
   691	   638	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   692	   639	    let code;
   693	   640	    await assert.doesNotReject(async () => { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); }, 'a stale branch must not crash the supervisor');
   694	   641	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a stale subtask branch parks the run');
   695	   642	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED);
   696	   643	  });
   697	   644	
   698	   645	  it('RED (G2): a second pipeline run RESUMES loadLoopState — cap counters survive re-invocation (no refund)', async () => {
   699	   646	    // A strict runner that NEVER falls back to default-APPROVED: it throws once
   700	   647	    // the script is exhausted, so a re-initing (refunding) impl is caught rather
   701	   648	    // than masked. The checkout -b branch state is neutralized by giving each run
   702	   649	    // a fresh git (a resumed run legitimately reuses persisted worktree state).
   703	   650	    const strictRunner = (io, script) => {
   704	   651	      const queue = [...script];
   705	   652	      const calls = [];
   706	   653	      const fn = (spec, opts) => {
   707	   654	        if (queue.length === 0) throw new Error('strictRunner: queue exhausted (no default-APPROVED fallback)');
   708	   655	        calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [] });
   709	   656	        const r = queue.shift();
   710	   657	        return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: r.verdict, findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   711	   658	      };
   712	   659	      fn.calls = calls;
   713	   660	      return fn;
   714	   661	    };
   715	   662	    const freshGit = () => { const g = pipelineGit(); g.setLockProbe(() => io.fs.existsSync(MERGE_LOCK)); return g; };
   716	   663	
   717	   664	    const spec = pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] });
   718	   665	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   719	   666	
   720	   667	    // Run 1 crashes at EXACTLY 3 persisted review iterations: writer+reviewer(BLOCKED)
   721	   668	    // x3, then the 4th writer spawn throws (a supervisor crash mid-run).
   722	   669	    io.execFile = freshGit();
   723	   670	    const runner1 = strictRunner(io, [
   724	   671	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' },
   725	   672	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' },
   726	   673	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' },
   727	   674	    ]);
   728	   675	    io.superviseChild = runner1;
   729	   676	    io.__runner = runner1;
   730	   677	    let firstCode = 'ran';
   731	   678	    try { firstCode = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { firstCode = 'crashed'; }
   732	   679	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations['subtask-t1-review'], 3, 'exactly 3 review iterations persisted before the crash');
   733	   680	
   734	   681	    // Run 2 resumes: two more BLOCKED reviews reach the 5-cap; the 6th escalates.
   735	   682	    // The strict runner throws on a 5th call, so a REFUNDING (re-init) impl — which
   736	   683	    // would need more attempts before escalating — fails to reach exit 3.
   737	   684	    io.execFile = freshGit();
   738	   685	    const runner2 = strictRunner(io, [
   739	   686	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, // iteration 4
   740	   687	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, // iteration 5 -> next spawn is refused by the cap
   741	   688	    ]);
   742	   689	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   743	   690	    io.__runner = runner2;
   744	   691	    let second = 'ran';
   745	   692	    try { second = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { second = 'crashed'; }
   746	   693	    assert.equal(second, 3, 'the cap counter survived re-invocation — cumulative attempts (3+2) escalate, not refund');
   747	   694	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED);
   748	   695	  });
   749	   696	});
   750	   697	
   751	   698	// ===========================================================================
   752	   699	// G3 (A3/B4) — pipeline consumes writer results/classification.
   753	   700	describe('pipeline — G3: writer result handling', () => {
   754	   701	  it('RED (G3): a writer BLOCKED retries the WRITER without spawning a reviewer for the failed attempt', async () => {
   755	   702	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   756	   703	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   757	   704	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'writer-not-done' }, // writer 1 fails its own self-check
   758	   705	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // writer retry
   759	   706	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   760	   707	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
   761	   708	    ]);
   762	   709	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   763	   710	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   764	   711	    // Two write-capable writer attempts ran; no reviewer ran until the writer passed.
   765	   712	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 2, 'the BLOCKED writer retried');
   766	   713	    // The FIRST reviewer spawn must come AFTER the second (passing) writer — never
   767	   714	    // a review of the first, failed writer attempt.
   768	   715	    const firstReviewerIdx = io.__runner.calls.findIndex((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only');
   769	   716	    const writerIdxs = io.__runner.calls.map((c, i) => (valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'workspace-write' ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
   770	   717	    assert.ok(firstReviewerIdx > writerIdxs[1], 'no reviewer spawned for the failed writer attempt (review only follows a passing writer)');
   771	   718	  });
   772	   719	
   773	   720	  it('RED (G3): a writer whose LOG carries the claude session-limit banner routes to failover (not a plain review)', async () => {
   774	   721	    const io = makePipeRepo({
   775	   722	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
   776	   723	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
   777	   724	      config: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-w', 'codex/cx-w@xhigh'] } },
   778	   725	      runner: undefined,
   779	   726	    });
   780	   727	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   781	   728	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `implementing...\n${CC_LIMIT}\n` }, // writer hits a limit
   782	   729	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched writer
   783	   730	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
   784	   731	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
   785	   732	    ]);
   786	   733	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   787	   734	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   788	   735	    // Failover was consulted: the relaunched writer ran on the OTHER platform
   789	   736	    // (codex/cx-w — the failover-resolved model), and the failed (limit) writer
   790	   737	    // NEVER handed off to a reviewer.
   791	   738	    const relaunch = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => c.command === 'codex' && argsOf(c).includes('cx-w'));
   792	   739	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'the limit death was classified and the writer relaunched on the failover model (codex/cx-w)');
   793	   740	    const firstReviewerIdx = io.__runner.calls.findIndex((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '-s') === 'read-only');
   794	   741	    const relaunchIdx = io.__runner.calls.indexOf(relaunch);
   795	   742	    assert.ok(firstReviewerIdx === -1 || firstReviewerIdx > relaunchIdx, 'the limit-failed writer never handed off to a reviewer before the relaunch');
   796	   743	  });
   797	   744	
   798	   745	  it('RED (G3): a writer that commits NOTHING (empty branch) is refused/parked — an empty branch never merges', async () => {
   799	   746	    // The stateful git returns an EMPTY main..branch log (no commits ahead).
   800	   747	    const git = pipelineGit({ log: '' });
   801	   748	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
   802	   749	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   803	   750	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   804	   751	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   805	   752	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'an empty subtask branch parks rather than merging nothing');
   806	   753	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'no empty branch is merged to main');
   807	   754	  });
   808	   755	});
   809	   756	
   810	   757	// ===========================================================================
   811	   758	// G5 (A5/B5) — reviewer-seat preflight + self-heal wiring.
   812	   759	describe('pipeline — G5: reviewer-seat preflight + self-heal', () => {
   813	   760	  it('RED (G5): a DIRTY reviewer seat parks BEFORE the reviewer spawns', async () => {
   814	   761	    // subtask 1 reviewer runs from wt-b; a dirty wt-b must be caught by preflight.
   815	   762	    const git = pipelineGit({ dirtyCwds: [WT_B] });
   816	   763	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
   817	   764	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   818	   765	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   819	   766	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   820	   767	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a dirty reviewer seat parks the run');
   821	   768	    assert.equal(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length, 0, 'the reviewer never spawned into a dirty seat');
   822	   769	  });
   823	   770	
   824	   771	  it('RED (G5): a listed-but-raw-deleted seat self-heals (prune + re-add), not a crash', async () => {
   825	   772	    // git genuinely LISTS wt-a (explicit seed) but its dir is raw-deleted, so any
   826	   773	    // in-cwd git in wt-a fails until self-heal prunes + re-adds it.
   827	   774	    const git = pipelineGit();
   828	   775	    git.seedWorktree(WT_A, 'baton/wt-a/base', { raw: true });
   829	   776	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
   830	   777	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   831	   778	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   832	   779	    let threw = false;
   833	   780	    try {
   834	   781	      await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   835	   782	    } catch {
   836	   783	      threw = true;
   837	   784	    }
   838	   785	    assert.equal(threw, false, 'a raw-deleted seat must self-heal, not crash the supervisor');
   839	   786	    assert.ok(io.__git.issued(/worktree prune/), 'the stale worktree metadata was pruned during self-heal');
   840	   787	    assert.ok(io.__git.matching(/worktree add/).some((c) => c.argstr.includes(WT_A)), 'the raw-deleted seat was re-added');
   841	   788	  });
   842	   789	});
   843	   790	
   844	   791	// A persisted loop-state fixture. `flavor`/`specDigest` are the state-binding
   845	   792	// stamp (H5 design): flavor ∈ {'loop','pipeline'}; specDigest = dedupeKey of the
   846	   793	// driving spec (subtasks for pipeline, phases for loop).
   847	   794	function seedPipelineState(io, over = {}) {
   848	   795	  const state = {
   849	   796	    schema: 'baton/loop-state@1',
   850	   797	    runId: 'loop-seeded',
   851	   798	    goal: 'Ship the pipeline',
   852	   799	    phaseCount: 2,
   853	   800	    phaseIndex: 0,
   854	   801	    iterations: {},
   855	   802	    status: 'running',
   856	   803	    parkReason: null,
   857	   804	    escalation: null,
   858	   805	    smokeApproval: null,
   859	   806	    createdAt: T0,
   860	   807	    journalSeq: 0,
   861	   808	    flavor: 'pipeline',
   862	   809	    ...over,
   863	   810	  };
   864	   811	  io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
   865	   812	  io.fs.writeFileSync(loopPaths('/repo').state, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2) + '\n');
   866	   813	  return state;
   867	   814	}
   868	   815	
   869	   816	// ===========================================================================
   870	   817	// H1 (B1) — the WRITER prompt must carry the verdict-tail contract.
   871	   818	describe('pipeline — H1: writer prompt carries the verdict-tail contract', () => {
   872	   819	  it('RED (H1): the writer prompt contains "End with exactly" and "VERDICT:" (a writer with no verdict parses BLOCKED-unparseable)', async () => {
   873	   820	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   874	   821	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   875	   822	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   876	   823	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   877	   824	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   878	   825	    assert.ok(writer, 'a writer child ran');
   879	   826	    const prompt = argsOf(writer).join(' ');
   880	   827	    assert.match(prompt, /End with exactly/, 'the writer prompt states the required tail');
   881	   828	    assert.match(prompt, /VERDICT:/, 'the writer prompt names the VERDICT line it must emit');
   882	   829	  });
   883	   830	});
   884	   831	
   885	   832	// ===========================================================================
   886	   833	// H2 (A1/B2) — bounded failover: avoidEntries/avoid carry; exhaustion PARKS.
   887	   834	describe('pipeline — H2: bounded failover (avoid carries; exhaustion parks)', () => {
   888	   835	  // A recording runner that emits a platform-appropriate death banner and THROWS
   889	   836	  // once a bound is exceeded, so an unbounded ping-pong terminates the test RED.
   890	   837	  function boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound, banner }) {
   891	   838	    const calls = [];
   892	   839	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   893	   840	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: (spec?.args ?? []).map(String) });
   894	   841	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} child spawns (ping-pong)`);
   895	   842	      const text = typeof banner === 'function' ? banner(spec) : banner;
   896	   843	      io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   897	   844	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working...\n${text}\n`);
   898	   845	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 1, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   899	   846	    };
   900	   847	    fn.calls = calls;
   901	   848	    return fn;
   902	   849	  }
   903	   850	  const modelsOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(c.args, '-s') === 'workspace-write').map((c) => valAfter(c.args, '--model'));
   904	   851	
   905	   852	  it('RED (H2a): always-model-unavailable on a two-entry codex chain never re-picks a rejected model; all rejected -> PARK (bounded)', async () => {
   906	   853	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
   907	   854	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
   908	   855	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 3, banner: MODEL_UNAVAIL }); // correct impl parks at 2 writers
   909	   856	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   910	   857	    io.__runner = runner;
   911	   858	    let code = 'ran';
   912	   859	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   913	   860	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'both models rejected -> the subtask parks (bounded, no infinite ping-pong)');
   914	   861	    // BOTH entries are attempted, in order, exactly once — then it parks.
   915	   862	    assert.deepEqual(modelsOf(runner), ['wa1', 'wa2'], `each chain entry is tried once, in order; got ${JSON.stringify(modelsOf(runner))}`);
   916	   863	    assert.ok(runner.calls.length <= 3, `the runner stayed within the throwing bound (no ping-pong); calls=${runner.calls.length}`);
   917	   864	  });
   918	   865	
   919	   866	  it('RED (H2b): always-usage-limit across a cross-platform chain parks within a bounded child budget (no infinite loop)', async () => {
   920	   867	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/wa-cc', 'codex/wa-cx@xhigh'] } };
   921	   868	    const io = makePipeRepo({
   922	   869	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
   923	   870	      config,
   924	   871	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
   925	   872	      runner: undefined,
   926	   873	    });
   927	   874	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 4, banner: (spec) => (spec.command === 'claude' ? CC_LIMIT : CODEX_LIMIT) });
   928	   875	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   929	   876	    io.__runner = runner;
   930	   877	    let code = 'ran';
   931	   878	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   932	   879	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a repeatedly-limit-dying subtask parks within the child budget, never loops forever');
   933	   880	  });
   934	   881	});
   935	   882	
   936	   883	// ===========================================================================
   937	   884	// D2 (dogfood milestone C) — `pipeline run` must actually PASS the main repo
   938	   885	// .git root to write-capable writer children: a linked worktree's index/lock
   939	   886	// live under <mainRoot>/.git/worktrees/<seat>, outside the seat-cwd sandbox
   940	   887	// (live child 002 could not commit: "sandbox only has read access to
   941	   888	// .git/worktrees/wt-a/index.lock"). The unit pin proves buildChildArgv honors
   942	   889	// opts.gitDir; THIS pin proves the command wires it through (pipeline.mjs:237
   943	   890	// still passes only {root: seatPath}).
   944	   891	describe('pipeline — D2: the writer spawn passes the main .git as a sandbox writable root', () => {
   945	   892	  // Every value that follows a `-c` flag in a codex argv (config args).
   946	   893	  const configArgs = (args) => args.map((a, i) => (a === '-c' ? args[i + 1] : null)).filter((v) => typeof v === 'string');
   947	   894	
   948	   895	  it('RED (D2): the write-capable writer child runs in its SEAT cwd yet gets the MAIN /repo/.git in sandbox_workspace_write.writable_roots', async () => {
   949	   896	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   950	   897	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
   951	   898	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   952	   899	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   953	   900	
   954	   901	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
   955	   902	    assert.ok(writer, 'a write-capable writer child ran');
   956	   903	    const args = argsOf(writer);
   957	   904	    // The cwd stays the seat worktree (codex passes it via -C) — the sandbox is
   958	   905	    // still rooted at the seat, the .git dir is only an ADDITIONAL writable root.
   959	   906	    assert.equal(valAfter(args, '-C'), WT_A, 'the writer runs in its seat worktree, not the main root');
   960	   907	    const wr = configArgs(args).find((v) => /sandbox_workspace_write\.writable_roots/.test(String(v)));
   961	   908	    assert.ok(wr, 'the writer argv carries a -c sandbox_workspace_write.writable_roots grant');
   962	   909	    assert.match(String(wr), /\/repo\/\.git/, 'the extra writable root is the MAIN repo .git dir (where the linked-worktree index/lock live)');
   963	   910	  });
   964	   911	});
   965	   912	
   966	   913	// ===========================================================================
   967	   914	// D1 (dogfood milestone C) — classification reads the transcript TAIL, not the
   968	   915	// whole log: a child's OWN work product (a README diff hunk that DOCUMENTS
   969	   916	// usage-limit failover) contains a verbatim signature mid-log. A healthy exit-0
   970	   917	// APPROVED writer must not be routed into failover because of a signature in the
   971	   918	// body it wrote. Death banners sit at the END of a log; a body hunk must not
   972	   919	// classify. Seam: the call site classifies a bounded tail (~4 KB) — pin the
   973	   920	// behavior, not the constant (the signature is placed FAR from the end).
   974	   921	describe('pipeline — D1: classification uses the transcript tail, not the body', () => {
   975	   922	  // A signature buried in the body, then >>4 KB of clean output, then a clean
   976	   923	  // APPROVED verdict tail — the real shape of a child that wrote about limits.
   977	   924	  const bodySignatureLog = (signature) =>
   978	   925	    `writing README.md…\n${signature}\n` +
   979	   926	    `diff --git a/README.md b/README.md\n` +
   980	   927	    '+ a clean line of the documented diff hunk\n'.repeat(600) + // ~24 KB of body
   981	   928	    `\ntokens used: 512\nDone — committed on the branch.\nVERDICT: APPROVED\nFINDINGS: none\n`;
   982	   929	
   983	   930	  it('RED (D1): a writer whose LOG BODY carries a usage-limit signature but whose TAIL is a clean exit-0 APPROVED is treated OK (proceeds to review, NO failover)', async () => {
   984	   931	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
   985	   932	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   986	   933	      // worker-a is codex/wa-model → a codex usage-limit signature in the body.
   987	   934	      { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0, logContent: bodySignatureLog(CODEX_LIMIT) },
   988	   935	      // reviewer + merger fall through to the default APPROVED.
   989	   936	    ]);
   990	   937	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   991	   938	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
   992	   939	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes — the body signature was NOT treated as a death; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   993	   940	    assert.equal(writersOf(io.__runner).length, 1, 'the writer ran exactly once — no failover relaunch on its own work product');
   994	   941	    assert.ok(reviewersOf(io.__runner).length >= 1, 'the run proceeded to review (the writer was classified OK)');
   995	   942	  });
   996	   943	
   997	   944	  it('GUARD (D1): a death banner at the END of the log still classifies and routes to failover', async () => {
   998	   945	    // The real death shape: clean body, banner in the TAIL. Model-unavailable
   999	   946	    // (codex) re-resolves the next chain entry with no bundle needed.
  1000	   947	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
  1001	   948	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1002	   949	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1003	   950	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `did some work…\nall clean\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // banner at the tail
  1004	   951	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched writer (wa2)
  1005	   952	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
  1006	   953	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
  1007	   954	    ]);
  1008	   955	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1009	   956	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1010	   957	    const relaunch = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => c.command === 'codex' && argsOf(c).includes('wa2'));
  1011	   958	    assert.ok(relaunch, 'a tail banner is still classified — the writer relaunched on the next chain entry (wa2)');
  1012	   959	  });
  1013	   960	});
  1014	   961	
  1015	   962	// ===========================================================================
  1016	   963	// D4 (dogfood milestone C) — a plain BLOCKED writer retry must not re-resolve
  1017	   964	// from the ORIGINAL chain, ignoring entries already avoided by failover.
  1018	   965	// subtaskAvoidEntries must feed EVERY writer resolution for the subtask, not
  1019	   966	// only runFailover. Live: after sol→gpt-5.5 relaunch, a BLOCKED retry reset the
  1020	   967	// writer to resolveOne('worker-a') = sol again (the avoided, dead entry).
  1021	   968	describe('pipeline — D4: avoided entries carry into a plain BLOCKED writer retry', () => {
  1022	   969	  const writerModels = (runner) => writersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1023	   970	
  1024	   971	  it('RED (D4): after a model-unavailable relaunch onto wa2, a later BLOCKED retry re-runs wa2 (NOT the avoided wa1) — exactly one wa1 attempt', async () => {
  1025	   972	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
  1026	   973	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1027	   974	    // wa1 ALWAYS dies model-unavailable; wa2 self-check-BLOCKs once, then APPROVES.
  1028	   975	    // reviewer (sr-model) and merger (mg-model) approve.
  1029	   976	    const wa2Queue = [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0 }, { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 }];
  1030	   977	    const calls = [];
  1031	   978	    const runner = (spec, opts) => {
  1032	   979	      const args = (spec?.args ?? []).map(String);
  1033	   980	      const model = args[args.indexOf('--model') + 1];
  1034	   981	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args, model });
  1035	   982	      let r;
  1036	   983	      if (model === 'wa1') {
  1037	   984	        io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
  1038	   985	        io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n`);
  1039	   986	        r = { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1 };
  1040	   987	      } else if (model === 'wa2') {
  1041	   988	        r = wa2Queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 };
  1042	   989	      } else {
  1043	   990	        r = { verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 };
  1044	   991	      }
  1045	   992	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict, findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
  1046	   993	    };
  1047	   994	    runner.calls = calls;
  1048	   995	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  1049	   996	    io.__runner = runner;
  1050	   997	
  1051	   998	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1052	   999	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes on wa2 after the BLOCKED retry; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1053	  1000	    const models = writerModels(runner);
  1054	  1001	    assert.equal(models.filter((m) => m === 'wa1').length, 1, `wa1 (avoided/dead) is attempted exactly once — the BLOCKED retry must re-pick wa2, not the original chain head; got ${JSON.stringify(models)}`);
  1055	  1002	    assert.ok(models.filter((m) => m === 'wa2').length >= 2, 'wa2 ran the BLOCKED attempt AND the passing retry');
  1056	  1003	  });
  1057	  1004	});
  1058	  1005	
  1059	  1006	// ===========================================================================
  1060	  1007	// D5 (dogfood milestone C) — the sessionHint passed to failover/checkpoint is
  1061	  1008	// the runId itself, with NO double 'loop-' prefix. Live artifact:
  1062	  1009	// 'loop-loop-6a96bf7b9ce6' (runId is already 'loop-<hex>').
  1063	  1010	describe('pipeline — D5: failover sessionHint is the runId (no double loop- prefix)', () => {
  1064	  1011	  it('RED (D5): after a usage-limit failover the received bundle is owned by the runId, not loop-<runId>', async () => {
  1065	  1012	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1066	  1013	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }),
  1067	  1014	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('claude-code', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' },
  1068	  1015	      config: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/cc-w', 'codex/cx-w@xhigh'] } },
  1069	  1016	      runner: undefined,
  1070	  1017	    });
  1071	  1018	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  1072	  1019	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `implementing…\n${CC_LIMIT}\n` }, // writer usage-limit → seal/receive
  1073	  1020	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // relaunched writer (cx-w)
  1074	  1021	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // reviewer
  1075	  1022	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // merger
  1076	  1023	    ]);
  1077	  1024	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1078	  1025	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1079	  1026	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  1080	  1027	    assert.match(runId, /^loop-/, 'sanity: the runId is already loop-prefixed');
  1081	  1028	    const bundle = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
  1082	  1029	    assert.equal(bundle.origin.sessionHint, runId, 'the failover/checkpoint session hint is the runId itself');
  1083	  1030	    assert.doesNotMatch(String(bundle.origin.sessionHint), /^loop-loop-/, 'no double loop- prefix (the live loop-loop-<hex> bug)');
  1084	  1031	  });
  1085	  1032	});
  1086	  1033	
  1087	  1034	// ===========================================================================
  1088	  1035	// D6 (dogfood milestone C, attempt 2) — reviewer and merger children get the
  1089	  1036	// SAME classify→failover treatment as writers. Live: the subtask-reviewer
  1090	  1037	// inherits the other seat's worker chain whose head was a dead model; each
  1091	  1038	// unparseable death counted as a BLOCKED review and burned the whole gate cap.
  1092	  1039	// A failure-class death must re-resolve the role (model-unavailable → entry
  1093	  1040	// avoid), NOT consume a gate iteration, and park on chain exhaustion.
  1094	  1041	describe('pipeline — D6: reviewer & merger get classify→failover like writers', () => {
  1095	  1042	  const MU_LOG = `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n`;
  1096	  1043	  const approve = () => ({ verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 });
  1097	  1044	  const deathMU = () => ({ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: MU_LOG });
  1098	  1045	  // A child runner keyed on the spawn's --model. handlers[model] is a function
  1099	  1046	  // called per spawn (closures model queues); the default is APPROVED. Throws
  1100	  1047	  // past `bound` so an unbounded re-resolution loop terminates the test RED.
  1101	  1048	  function byModelRunner(io, handlers, { bound = 40 } = {}) {
  1102	  1049	    const calls = [];
  1103	  1050	    const fn = (spec, opts) => {
  1104	  1051	      const args = (spec?.args ?? []).map(String);
  1105	  1052	      const model = args[args.indexOf('--model') + 1];
  1106	  1053	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args, model });
  1107	  1054	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} spawns`);
  1108	  1055	      const h = handlers[model] ?? approve;
  1109	  1056	      const r = h();
  1110	  1057	      if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
  1111	  1058	        io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
  1112	  1059	        io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
  1113	  1060	      }
  1114	  1061	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
  1115	  1062	    };
  1116	  1063	    fn.calls = calls;
  1117	  1064	    return fn;
  1118	  1065	  }
  1119	  1066	  const reviewerModels = (runner) => reviewersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1120	  1067	  const mergersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1121	  1068	  const mergerModels = (runner) => mergersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1122	  1069	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1123	  1070	  const mergeReceipts = (io) => {
  1124	  1071	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1125	  1072	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1126	  1073	  };
  1127	  1074	  // Transitions are journaled — a re-resolved death must leave NO gate-iteration
  1128	  1075	  // event, not merely a snapshot counter that happens to read 0.
  1129	  1076	  const gateIterationEvents = (io, gate) => {
  1130	  1077	    const raw = io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal];
  1131	  1078	    const events = typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1132	  1079	    return events.filter((e) => e.type === 'gate-iteration' && e.gate === gate);
  1133	  1080	  };
  1134	  1081	
  1135	  1082	  it('RED (D6a): a model-unavailable subtask-reviewer re-resolves the next chain entry (one r1 then r2), no gate burn, run DONE', async () => {
  1136	  1083	    // subtask t1 uses seat a; the reviewer draws the OTHER seat's chain (worker-b).
  1137	  1084	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa-model@xhigh'], 'worker-b': ['codex/r1@xhigh', 'codex/r2@xhigh'] } };
  1138	  1085	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
  1139	  1086	    const runner = byModelRunner(io, { 'wa-model': approve, r1: deathMU, r2: approve, 'mg-model': approve });
  1140	  1087	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  1141	  1088	    io.__runner = runner;
  1142	  1089	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1143	  1090	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the review succeeds via re-resolution; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1144	  1091	    assert.deepEqual(reviewerModels(runner), ['r1', 'r2'], `the dead reviewer entry re-resolved to the next; got ${JSON.stringify(reviewerModels(runner))}`);
  1145	  1092	    const state = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state;
  1146	  1093	    assert.equal(state.iterations['subtask-t1-review'] ?? 0, 0, 'a model-unavailable reviewer death did NOT burn a gate iteration');

  1149	  1096	  });
  1150	  1097	
  1151	  1098	  it('RED (D6b): a model-unavailable merger re-resolves the next chain entry (one m1 then m2), the merge lands, run DONE', async () => {

  1270	  1217	
  1271	  1218	// ===========================================================================
  1272	  1219	// ITEM 4 (v1.1) — findings persistence (pipeline half). A re-invoke re-prompts
  1273	  1220	// the gate's writer with the LATEST persisted findings, and a cap escalation
  1274	  1221	// embeds them even when the in-memory findings are empty (preferred over the D8
  1275	  1222	// log-tail fallback).
  1276	  1223	describe('pipeline — findings persistence (item 4)', () => {
  1277	  1224	  const FDIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/findings`;
  1278	  1225	  const ESC = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/ESCALATION.md`;
  1279	  1226	  // Two ordered persisted lines — the LATEST must win; the older must not be
  1280	  1227	  // selected as the active prior findings.
  1281	  1228	  const OLD = 'OLDER_FINDINGS_SUPERSEDED_PIPE';
  1282	  1229	  const NEW = 'NEWER_FINDINGS_LATEST_PIPE';
  1283	  1230	  const seedTwoFindings = (io, gate) => {
  1284	  1231	    io.fs.mkdirSync(FDIR, { recursive: true });
  1285	  1232	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${FDIR}/${gate}.ndjson`,
  1286	  1233	      JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: OLD, at: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ iteration: 2, findings: NEW, at: T0 }) + '\n');
  1287	  1234	  };
  1288	  1235	
  1289	  1236	  it('RED (4b pipeline): a re-invoke embeds the LATEST persisted findings in the FIRST writer prompt (older superseded)', async () => {
  1290	  1237	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1291	  1238	    const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1292	  1239	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1293	  1240	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1294	  1241	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1295	  1242	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', iterations: { [gate]: 2 } });
  1296	  1243	    seedTwoFindings(io, gate);
  1297	  1244	
  1298	  1245	    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1299	  1246	    const writer = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1300	  1247	    assert.ok(writer, 'a writer spawned on re-invoke');
  1301	  1248	    const prompt = argsOf(writer).join(' ');
  1302	  1249	    assert.match(prompt, new RegExp(NEW), 'the first writer prompt embeds the LATEST persisted findings (not an empty string)');
  1303	  1250	    assert.doesNotMatch(prompt, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not selected as the active prior findings');
  1304	  1251	  });
  1305	  1252	
  1306	  1253	  it('RED (4c pipeline): a fresh-invoke cap escalation embeds the LATEST PERSISTED findings, not the D8 log tail', async () => {
  1307	  1254	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1308	  1255	    const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1309	  1256	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1310	  1257	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks, budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }),
  1311	  1258	      runner: undefined,
  1312	  1259	    });
  1313	  1260	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // unused — escalates before spawn
  1314	  1261	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1315	  1262	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', iterations: { [gate]: 1 } });
  1316	  1263	    seedTwoFindings(io, gate);
  1317	  1264	
  1318	  1265	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1319	  1266	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a gate at the cap escalates');
  1320	  1267	    const esc = io.files()[ESC];
  1321	  1268	    assert.match(esc, new RegExp(NEW), 'the cap escalation embeds the LATEST persisted findings even when in-memory findings are empty');
  1322	  1269	    assert.doesNotMatch(esc, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not the one embedded');
  1323	  1270	    // Persisted genuinely WINS over the D8 fallback — no log-tail footer when
  1324	  1271	    // persisted findings exist.
  1325	  1272	    assert.doesNotMatch(esc, /Last child log tail|produced no parseable findings/, 'the D8 empty-findings log-tail footer is absent when persisted findings exist');
  1326	  1273	  });
  1327	  1274	});
  1328	  1275	
  1329	  1276	// ===========================================================================
  1330	  1277	// ITEM 4b (v1.1) — review artifacts, pipeline half. Multi-child gates encode the
  1331	  1278	// child ROLE in the filename: reviews/<runId>/subtask-<id>-review/iteration-NN/
  1332	  1279	// <role>.prompt.md + <role>.verdict.md, role ∈ writer|reviewer|merger (the
  1333	  1280	// writer self-check verdict included). NN = the gate iteration count at the
  1334	  1281	// WRITER spawn of the cycle (01-based) — all three children share it.
  1335	  1282	describe('pipeline — review artifacts (item 4b)', () => {
  1336	  1283	  const promptOf = (call) => (argsOf(call) ?? []).find((a) => a.includes('You are the')) ?? '';
  1337	  1284	  const parseVerdictMd = (text) => {
  1338	  1285	    const lines = String(text ?? '').split('\n');
  1339	  1286	    const header = /** @type {Record<string,string>} */ ({});
  1340	  1287	    let i = 0;
  1341	  1288	    for (; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  1342	  1289	      const m = lines[i].match(/^([A-Za-z][\w-]*):\s?(.*)$/);
  1343	  1290	      if (!m) break;
  1344	  1291	      header[m[1].toLowerCase()] = m[2].trim();
  1345	  1292	    }
  1346	  1293	    return { header, body: lines.slice(i).join('\n').trim() };
  1347	  1294	  };
  1348	  1295	
  1349	  1296	  it('RED (4b pipeline): a clean subtask cycle writes writer/reviewer/merger prompt+verdict artifacts under iteration-01', async () => {
  1350	  1297	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1351	  1298	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  1352	  1299	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // writer, reviewer, merger — all APPROVED
  1353	  1300	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1354	  1301	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1355	  1302	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the subtask completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1356	  1303	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  1357	  1304	    const base = `/repo/reviews/${runId}/subtask-t1-review/iteration-01`;
  1358	  1305	    const f = (rel) => io.files()[`${base}/${rel}`];
  1359	  1306	
  1360	  1307	    const writerCall = writersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1361	  1308	    const reviewerCall = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1362	  1309	    const mergerCall = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1363	  1310	    for (const [role, call, model] of [['writer', writerCall, 'wa-model'], ['reviewer', reviewerCall, 'wb-model'], ['merger', mergerCall, 'mg-model']]) {
  1364	  1311	      assert.ok(call, `${role} child ran`);
  1365	  1312	      assert.ok(f(`${role}.prompt.md`), `${role}.prompt.md written under iteration-01`);
  1366	  1313	      assert.ok(f(`${role}.verdict.md`), `${role}.verdict.md written under iteration-01`);
  1367	  1314	      assert.equal(f(`${role}.prompt.md`), promptOf(call), `${role}.prompt.md === the ${role} prompt verbatim`);
  1368	  1315	      const v = parseVerdictMd(f(`${role}.verdict.md`));
  1369	  1316	      assert.equal(v.header.verdict, 'APPROVED', `${role} verdict header: exact verdict`);
  1370	  1317	      assert.match(v.header.model ?? '', new RegExp(model), `${role} verdict header: concrete model`);
  1371	  1318	      assert.match(v.header.date ?? '', /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, `${role} verdict header: date`);
  1372	  1319	      assert.ok('degraded' in v.header, `${role} verdict header: degraded field present`);
  1373	  1320	    }
  1374	  1321	  });
  1375	  1322	});
  1376	  1323	
  1377	  1324	// ===========================================================================
  1378	  1325	// ITEM 9 (v1.1) — pipeline smoke gate. After the FINAL subtask merges, a
  1379	  1326	// pipeline whose loop.json sets smoke.cmd reuses the loop's smoke machinery
  1380	  1327	// (smokeApprovalToken/verifySmokeToken, SMOKE_AWAIT/SMOKE_APPROVE, --approve-smoke):
  1381	  1328	// run smoke.cmd, await a drift-bound token, complete only on a verified token.
  1382	  1329	describe('pipeline — smoke gate (item 9)', () => {
  1383	  1330	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  1384	  1331	  // A git that also answers the smoke command (non-git → 'ok'), so merges work
  1385	  1332	  // AND the smoke.cmd runs through io.execFile.
  1386	  1333	  const smokeAwareIo = (spec, { git = pipelineGit() } = {}) => {
  1387	  1334	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec, git, runner: undefined });
  1388	  1335	    const baseExec = io.execFile;
  1389	  1336	    io.execFile = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) =>
  1390	  1337	      cmd === 'git' ? baseExec(cmd, args, opts) : Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
  1391	  1338	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1392	  1339	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1393	  1340	    return io;
  1394	  1341	  };
  1395	  1342	  const smokeSpec = () => pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }], smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' } });
  1396	  1343	
  1397	  1344	  it('RED (9-1): a pipeline with smoke.cmd AWAITS approval after the last subtask merges (not straight to DONE)', async () => {
  1398	  1345	    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
  1399	  1346	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1400	  1347	    assert.equal(code, 0, `awaiting smoke approval is a clean exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1401	  1348	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run awaits smoke approval, not DONE');
  1402	  1349	    assert.ok(io.files()[`${DIR}/merges.ndjson`]?.includes('t1'), 'the subtask merged BEFORE the smoke gate');
  1403	  1350	    assert.ok(io.files()[`${DIR}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md`], 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
  1404	  1351	    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
  1405	  1352	    assert.ok(approval, 'smoke-approval.json was written');
  1406	  1353	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  1407	  1354	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'a drift-bound smk token is issued');
  1408	  1355	  });
  1409	  1356	
  1410	  1357	  it('RED (9-2): pipeline run --approve-smoke <token> verifies and completes the run DONE', async () => {
  1411	  1358	    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec());
  1412	  1359	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
  1413	  1360	    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
  1414	  1361	    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 reached the smoke gate and issued a token');
  1415	  1362	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  1416	  1363	
  1417	  1364	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
  1418	  1365	    assert.equal(code, 0, `an approved pipeline completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1419	  1366	    const raw = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1420	  1367	    assert.equal(raw.status, 'done', 'the approved run persists status done');
  1421	  1368	  });
  1422	  1369	
  1423	  1370	  it('RED (9-3): a DRIFTED smoke token is refused and the pipeline PARKS (exit 4)', async () => {
  1424	  1371	    const git = pipelineGit();
  1425	  1372	    const io = smokeAwareIo(smokeSpec(), { git });
  1426	  1373	    assert.equal(await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io), 0);
  1427	  1374	    const approval = io.files()[`${DIR}/smoke-approval.json`];
  1428	  1375	    assert.ok(approval, 'run 1 issued a token');
  1429	  1376	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  1430	  1377	    // A bound input drifts: main moves after the human looked.
  1431	  1378	    git.moveMain('DRIFTED-SHA-9-3-AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA');
  1432	  1379	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
  1433	  1380	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a drifted token parks the pipeline (exit 4)');
  1434	  1381	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run');
  1435	  1382	  });
  1436	  1383	
  1437	  1384	  it('GUARD (9-4): a pipeline with NO smoke.cmd completes straight to DONE (no regression)', async () => {

  1626	  1573	
  1627	  1574	// ===========================================================================
  1628	  1575	// ITEM 2 (v1.1) — trunk derivation. Replace hardcoded 'main' with the repo's
  1629	  1576	// actual default branch (git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → fallback
  1630	  1577	// rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD at setup), recorded in state and reused, never
  1631	  1578	// re-derived mid-run. Pinned in a MASTER-trunk fake repo — the observable is
  1632	  1579	// "master used everywhere; one derivation", not the exact command.
  1633	  1580	
  1634	  1581	// A fake git modeling a MASTER-trunk repo: the default branch is 'master'
  1635	  1582	// (symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → origin/master; fallback rev-parse
  1636	  1583	// --abbrev-ref HEAD at the ROOT → master). It answers 'main' refs LENIENTLY
  1637	  1584	// (same sha/ranges) so a main-hardcoded impl still runs end to end — the pin's
  1638	  1585	// teeth are that NO 'main' appears in the recorded git argv. deriveThrows makes
  1639	  1586	// the default-branch derivation fail (to prove a resume reuses a stamped trunk).
  1640	  1587	// Structured argv classifiers shared by the fake's dispatch AND its call-count
  1641	  1588	// helpers, so both agree on what "a trunk-derivation probe" is (verifier iter 3):
  1642	  1589	// any arg order, plus symbolic-ref HEAD and branch --show-current variants.
  1643	  1590	function isDerivationShape(args) {
  1644	  1591	  const has = (a) => args.includes(a);
  1645	  1592	  return (
  1646	  1593	    (has('symbolic-ref') && (has('refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') || has('HEAD'))) || // (a) + (d)
  1647	  1594	    (has('rev-parse') && has('--abbrev-ref') && has('HEAD')) || // (b) any order
  1648	  1595	    (has('branch') && has('--show-current')) // (c)
  1649	  1596	  );
  1650	  1597	}
  1651	  1598	// The one legit exception: rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD (any order) in a seat cwd.
  1652	  1599	function isSeatHeadProbe(args, cwd) {
  1653	  1600	  const seat = cwd === WT_A || cwd === WT_B;
  1654	  1601	  return seat && args.includes('rev-parse') && args.includes('--abbrev-ref') && args.includes('HEAD');
  1655	  1602	}
  1656	  1603	
  1657	  1604	function masterGit({ merged = [], existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], log = CLEAN_LOG, deriveThrows = false } = {}) {
  1658	  1605	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
  1659	  1606	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
  1660	  1607	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches);
  1661	  1608	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds);
  1662	  1609	  const mergedSet = new Set(merged);
  1663	  1610	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  1664	  1611	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
  1665	  1612	  const list = () => {
  1666	  1613	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'master' }];
  1667	  1614	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
  1668	  1615	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
  1669	  1616	  };
  1670	  1617	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
  1671	  1618	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
  1672	  1619	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
  1673	  1620	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
  1674	  1621	    // ---- structured trunk-derivation classification (verifier iter 3) --------
  1675	  1622	    // Every current-/default-branch probe shape is classified by ARGV, not by an
  1676	  1623	    // exact string, so arg-order variants and alternate commands can't slip past:
  1677	  1624	    //   (a) symbolic-ref naming refs/remotes/origin/HEAD
  1678	  1625	    //   (b) rev-parse containing BOTH --abbrev-ref and HEAD (any order)
  1679	  1626	    //   (c) branch --show-current
  1680	  1627	    //   (d) symbolic-ref HEAD
  1681	  1628	    // The ONLY legit exception is form (b) issued in a seat cwd (the pipeline's
  1682	  1629	    // alreadyOnBranch / preflight / postflight HEAD checks).
  1683	  1630	    const seatCwd = opts?.cwd === WT_A || opts?.cwd === WT_B;
  1684	  1631	    if (isSeatHeadProbe(args, opts?.cwd)) {
  1685	  1632	      const dflt = opts.cwd === WT_A ? 'baton/wt-a/base' : 'baton/wt-b/base'; // never 'master' — a seat probe is never the trunk
  1686	  1633	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts.cwd) ?? dflt}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1687	  1634	    }
  1688	  1635	    if (isDerivationShape(args) && !seatCwd) {
  1689	  1636	      // A derivation attempt (root fallback or a stray probe in any non-seat cwd).
  1690	  1637	      // deriveThrows makes EVERY such form fail so it can't silently succeed via a
  1691	  1638	      // generic catch-all; otherwise it returns the shape-appropriate default.
  1692	  1639	      if (deriveThrows) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('fatal: no default branch'), { code: 128, stderr: 'fatal: no default branch\n' }));
  1693	  1640	      if (args.includes('symbolic-ref')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${args.includes('refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') ? 'refs/remotes/origin/master' : 'refs/heads/master'}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1694	  1641	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'master\n', stderr: '' }); // rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD / branch --show-current
  1695	  1642	    }
  1696	  1643	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
  1697	  1644	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
  1698	  1645	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
  1699	  1646	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
  1700	  1647	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
  1701	  1648	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); }
  1702	  1649	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1703	  1650	    }
  1704	  1651	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune') || argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1705	  1652	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
  1706	  1653	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
  1707	  1654	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
  1708	  1655	      branches.add(target);
  1709	  1656	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
  1710	  1657	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1711	  1658	    }
  1712	  1659	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
  1713	  1660	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> <trunk>: exit 0 iff branch already merged.
  1714	  1661	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
  1715	  1662	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
  1716	  1663	      return mergedSet.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
  1717	  1664	    }
  1718	  1665	    // Lenient: rev-parse of ANY single ref (master OR main) resolves to the sha.
  1719	  1666	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${MAIN_SHA}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1720	  1667	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
  1721	  1668	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
  1722	  1669	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
  1723	  1670	      if (branch && mergedSet.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); // merged → empty
  1724	  1671	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
  1725	  1672	    }
  1726	  1673	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' }); // merge <branch> and merge --ff-only <trunk>
  1727	  1674	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1728	  1675	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
  1729	  1676	  };
  1730	  1677	  fn.calls = calls;
  1731	  1678	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
  1732	  1679	  fn.mainCalls = () => fn.git().filter((c) => c.argstr.includes('main'));
  1733	  1680	  // A trunk-derivation attempt = ANY derivation SHAPE (symbolic-ref origin/HEAD,
  1734	  1681	  // rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD in any order, branch --show-current, symbolic-ref
  1735	  1682	  // HEAD) that is NOT the legit seat HEAD probe. Counts seat-cwd re-derivations of
  1736	  1683	  // other shapes too — the only exclusion is the explicit seat HEAD-probe form —
  1737	  1684	  // so "derive once, never re-derive" is pinned by the count, arg-order-proof.
  1738	  1685	  fn.derivationCalls = () => fn.git().filter((c) => isDerivationShape(c.args) && !isSeatHeadProbe(c.args, c.cwd)).length;
  1739	  1686	  // The legit seat HEAD probes (alreadyOnBranch / preflight / postflight),
  1740	  1687	  // confined to the two seat cwds — asserted explicitly.
  1741	  1688	  fn.seatProbes = () => fn.git().filter((c) => isSeatHeadProbe(c.args, c.cwd));
  1742	  1689	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
  1743	  1690	  return fn;
  1744	  1691	}
  1745	  1692	
  1746	  1693	describe('pipeline — trunk derivation (v1.1 item 2)', () => {
  1747	  1694	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1748	  1695	  const mergeReceiptsOf = (io) => {
  1749	  1696	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1750	  1697	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1751	  1698	  };
  1752	  1699	
  1753	  1700	  it('RED (trunk-1): a master-trunk pipeline completes DONE using master everywhere — no "main" in any git argv; prompts range over master', async () => {
  1754	  1701	    const git = masterGit();
  1755	  1702	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
  1756	  1703	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1757	  1704	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1758	  1705	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1759	  1706	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a master-trunk run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1760	  1707	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
  1761	  1708	    // Teeth: NO trunk touchpoint used the hardcoded 'main'.
  1762	  1709	    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'every trunk git call targets master — no main anywhere');
  1763	  1710	    // Reviewer prompt ranges over master..branch.
  1764	  1711	    const rev = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
  1765	  1712	    assert.ok(rev, 'a reviewer ran');
  1766	  1713	    const revPrompt = argsOf(rev).join(' ');
  1767	  1714	    assert.match(revPrompt, /git diff master\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the reviewer prompt ranges over master..<branch>');
  1768	  1715	    assert.doesNotMatch(revPrompt, /\bmain\.\./, 'the reviewer prompt never ranges over main');
  1769	  1716	    // Merger prompt names master as the trunk.
  1770	  1717	    const merger = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1771	  1718	    assert.ok(merger, 'a merger ran');
  1772	  1719	    assert.match(argsOf(merger).join(' '), /against master/, 'the merger prompt names master as the trunk');
  1773	  1720	    // Receipt shape unchanged.
  1774	  1721	    assert.ok(
  1775	  1722	      mergeReceiptsOf(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1' && r.branch === 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1' && typeof r.mergedAt === 'string'),
  1776	  1723	      'the merge receipt keeps its {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} shape',
  1777	  1724	    );
  1778	  1725	  });
  1779	  1726	
  1780	  1727	  it('RED (trunk-2): the stale-branch park message names the trunk (git log master..<branch>), not main', async () => {
  1781	  1728	    const branch = 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1';
  1782	  1729	    const git = masterGit({ merged: [branch] }); // is-ancestor true, empty master..branch, and NO receipt → stale park
  1783	  1730	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
  1784	  1731	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1785	  1732	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1786	  1733	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1787	  1734	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'an ancestor-without-receipt stale branch parks');
  1788	  1735	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  1789	  1736	    assert.match(out, /git log master\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the stale-park message points at git log master..<branch>');
  1790	  1737	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /\bmain\.\./, 'the stale-park message never ranges over main');
  1791	  1738	  });
  1792	  1739	
  1793	  1740	  it('RED (trunk-3a): a fresh master-trunk run STAMPS the derived trunk into state.json', async () => {
  1794	  1741	    const git = masterGit();
  1795	  1742	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
  1796	  1743	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());

  1800	  1747	    assert.equal(state.trunk, 'master', 'the derived trunk is persisted in state.json for reuse');
  1801	  1748	    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 1, 'the trunk is derived EXACTLY once at init (one root-level derivation)');
  1802	  1749	  });
  1803	  1750	
  1804	  1751	  it('RED (trunk-3b): resume REUSES the stamped trunk and does not re-derive (completes even when derivation now fails)', async () => {
  1805	  1752	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1806	  1753	    const git = masterGit({ deriveThrows: true }); // deriving would fail now — the resume must not need it
  1807	  1754	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1808	  1755	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1809	  1756	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1810	  1757	    // A prior run already derived + stamped the trunk.
  1811	  1758	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', trunk: 'master' });
  1812	  1759	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1813	  1760	    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume completes using the stamped trunk without re-deriving; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1814	  1761	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed run reached DONE');
  1815	  1762	    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'the resumed run uses the stamped master everywhere');
  1816	  1763	    // Derive-once teeth: a stamped resume issues NO derivation — not symbolic-ref,
  1817	  1764	    // not a root fallback, and not a stray abbrev-ref in any non-seat cwd (a
  1818	  1765	    // seat-cwd re-derivation would count here too, since only WT_A/WT_B probes
  1819	  1766	    // are excluded).
  1820	  1767	    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 0, 'a stamped resume NEVER re-derives the trunk (not even to catch-and-fallback, and not via a seat cwd)');
  1821	  1768	    // The exclusion is explicit: the only abbrev-ref HEAD calls are the known
  1822	  1769	    // legit seat probes, confined to the two seat cwds.
  1823	  1770	    assert.ok(git.seatProbes().length >= 1, 'the resume made its legit seat HEAD probes');
  1824	  1771	    assert.ok(git.seatProbes().every((c) => c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B), 'every abbrev-ref HEAD probe is a seat probe (WT_A/WT_B), never a disguised derivation');
  1825	  1772	  });
  1826	  1773	
  1827	  1774	  it('RED (trunk-4): a stamped state WITHOUT a trunk field migrates — derives once and stamps it, never a refusal', async () => {
  1828	  1775	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];

  1834	  1781	    // Missing trunk alone is NOT the I3 legacy-unstamped refusal.
  1835	  1782	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running' });
  1836	  1783	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1837	  1784	    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'a missing trunk field is additive migration, not a legacy-unstamped refusal');

  1839	  1786	    const state = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1840	  1787	    assert.equal(state.trunk, 'master', 'the trunk is derived once and stamped on migration');
  1841	  1788	    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 1, 'migration derives the trunk EXACTLY once, then stamps it');
  1842	  1789	  });

  1844	  1791	  it('RED (trunk-5): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask auto-advances under master (no t1 writer), zero main in argv', async () => {
  1845	  1792	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1846	  1793	    const t1Branch = 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1';

  1856	  1803

  1859	  1806	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed merged subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale under master');

  1864	  1811	    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'the already-merged recognizer and merge path use master — no main anywhere');
  1865	  1812	  });
  1866	  1813	});

  1869	  1816	// H5 (B4) — state flavor + spec binding across loop/pipeline.
  1870	  1817	describe('pipeline — H5: state flavor + spec-digest binding', () => {
  1871	  1818	  it('RED (H5a): a state.json created by `loop run` (flavor loop) is REFUSED by pipeline run (exit 2 naming the mismatch), zero spawns', async () => {
  1872	  1819	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });

  1882	  1829	  it('RED (H5b): a changed subtasks list (stale specDigest) on resume REFUSES (exit 2), rather than misaligning phaseIndex', async () => {

  1894	  1841	  });

  1896	  1843	
  1897	  1844	// ===========================================================================
  1898	  1845	// I1 (A1/B1) — auto-advance requires a supervisor-owned MERGE RECEIPT, not just
  1899	  1846	// is-ancestor: a stale empty branch at main's tip is is-ancestor-true too, and
  1900	  1847	// must PARK, not silently skip the subtask.
  1901	  1848	//
  1902	  1849	// Merge-receipt shape (I1 design): append-only `.handoff/loop/merges.ndjson`,
  1903	  1850	// one line per merged subtask `{ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt }`. Auto-advance on
  1904	  1851	// resume requires is-ancestor(branch, main) AND a receipt for that subtask.
  1905	  1852	describe('pipeline — I1: merge-receipt-gated auto-advance', () => {
  1906	  1853	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1907	  1854	  const seedReceipt = (io, subtaskId, branch) => {
  1908	  1855	    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
  1909	  1856	    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId, branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  1910	  1857	  };
  1911	  1858	  const receipts = (io) => {
  1912	  1859	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1913	  1860	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1914	  1861	  };
  1915	  1862	
  1916	  1863	  it('RED (I1b): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask ADVANCES on resume (no writer, no empty-branch park)', async () => {
  1917	  1864	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1918	  1865	    // t1 is genuinely merged: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1, AND a receipt.
  1919	  1866	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
  1920	  1867	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1921	  1868	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only
  1922	  1869	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1923	  1870	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1924	  1871	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1');
  1925	  1872	
  1926	  1873	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1927	  1874	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a receipt-backed merged subtask advances and the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1928	  1875	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale');
  1929	  1876	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1930	  1877	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
  1931	  1878	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — t1 was recognized as merged (receipt) and skipped');
  1932	  1879	  });
  1933	  1880	
  1934	  1881	  it('RED (I1a): a stale EMPTY branch at main tip (is-ancestor true, NO receipt) PARKS as stale — never auto-advances', async () => {
  1935	  1882	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1936	  1883	    // t1's branch is empty at main's tip: is-ancestor true, empty main..t1 — but
  1937	  1884	    // NO merge receipt (the writer crashed before its first commit).
  1938	  1885	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: ['baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'] });
  1939	  1886	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1940	  1887	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1941	  1888	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1942	  1889	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1943	  1890	    // No receipt seeded.
  1944	  1891	
  1945	  1892	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1946	  1893	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a receipt-less is-ancestor branch parks as stale rather than silently skipping the subtask');
  1947	  1894	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  1948	  1895	    assert.match(out, /stale/i, 'the park names the stale branch');
  1949	  1896	    assert.equal(io.__git.matching(/^merge baton\//).length, 0, 'nothing is merged for a stale empty branch');
  1950	  1897	    // J2: the message must hedge BOTH causes — each pinned SEPARATELY so a
  1951	  1898	    // single-cause misdiagnosis cannot pass — point at the range to inspect, and
  1952	  1899	    // account for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat (a bare
  1953	  1900	    // "delete the branch" fails while it is checked out).
  1954	  1901	    assert.match(out, /crashed before committing|before.*first commit|pre-?commit/i, 'names the pre-commit-crash cause');
  1955	  1902	    assert.match(out, /merged without a receipt|merge.*receipt.*missing|not.*recorded/i, 'ALSO names the merged-without-receipt cause (no single-cause misdiagnosis)');
  1956	  1903	    assert.match(out, /git log main\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'points at `git log main..<branch>` to disambiguate');
  1957	  1904	    assert.match(out, /seat|worktree|checked out|checkout/i, 'remediation accounts for the branch still being checked out in the writer seat');
  1958	  1905	  });
  1959	  1906	
  1960	  1907	  it('GUARD (I1 conjunction): a receipt WITHOUT is-ancestor does NOT auto-advance — the subtask is re-run, never silently skipped', async () => {
  1961	  1908	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
  1962	  1909	    // A receipt exists for t1, but the branch is NOT an ancestor of main (the
  1963	  1910	    // merge did not actually land — e.g. main was rewound). A receipt-ONLY
  1964	  1911	    // advance would skip t1; the required conjunction re-runs it.
  1965	  1912	    const git = pipelineGit({ mergedBranches: [] }); // is-ancestor FALSE for every branch
  1966	  1913	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
  1967	  1914	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [...cleanSubtask(), ...cleanSubtask()]);
  1968	  1915	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1969	  1916	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2 });
  1970	  1917	    seedReceipt(io, 't1', 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1'); // receipt present, ancestor absent
  1971	  1918	
  1972	  1919	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1973	  1920	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes by re-running t1; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  1974	  1921	    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
  1975	  1922	    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'a writer ran');
  1976	  1923	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_A, 'the FIRST writer is t1 (wt-a) — a receipt alone did NOT skip it (is-ancestor is a required conjunct)');
  1977	  1924	  });
  1978	  1925	
  1979	  1926	  it('RED (I1c): a clean merge PERSISTS a durable receipt {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} BEFORE the phase-advance is journaled', async () => {
  1980	  1927	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), runner: undefined });
  1981	  1928	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1982	  1929	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1983	  1930	
  1984	  1931	    // Ordering hook: at each phase-advance journal write, capture whether the t1
  1985	  1932	    // receipt is ALREADY on disk (durable before the advance, not merely by EOR).
  1986	  1933	    const journalPath = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/journal.ndjson`;
  1987	  1934	    const receiptAtAdvance = [];
  1988	  1935	    const realAppend = io.fs.appendFileSync.bind(io.fs);
  1989	  1936	    io.fs.appendFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ path, /** @type {any} */ data) => {
  1990	  1937	      if (String(path) === journalPath && /"type":"phase-advance"/.test(String(data))) {
  1991	  1938	        receiptAtAdvance.push(receipts(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1'));
  1992	  1939	      }
  1993	  1940	      return realAppend(path, data);
  1994	  1941	    };
  1995	  1942	
  1996	  1943	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1997	  1944	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  1998	  1945	    const rec = receipts(io).find((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1');
  1999	  1946	    assert.ok(rec, 'a merge receipt is persisted after the subtask merges');
  2000	  1947	    assert.equal(rec.branch, 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1', 'the receipt names the merged branch');
  2001	  1948	    assert.ok(typeof rec.mergedAt === 'string' && rec.mergedAt.length > 0, 'the receipt records mergedAt');
  2002	  1949	    assert.ok(receiptAtAdvance.length >= 1 && receiptAtAdvance.every(Boolean), 'the receipt is durable on disk BEFORE the phase advance is journaled (crash-safe)');
  2003	  1950	  });
  2004	  1951	});

  2010	  1957	  it('RED (I3): an unstamped state.json (no flavor/specDigest) refuses exit 2 with the archive instruction, zero spawns', async () => {

  2035	  1982	
  2036	  1983	  it('RED (J1a): resume transitions a PARKED run to running — the same subtask re-runs its writer, gate counters intact (no refund)', async () => {
  2037	  1984	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  2038	  1985	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), runner: undefined });
  2039	  1986	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  2040	  1987	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2041	  1988	    // A parked run mid-subtask t1 with the gate already iterated 3 times.
  2042	  1989	    seedPipelineState(io, {
  2043	  1990	      flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1,
  2044	  1991	      status: 'parked', parkReason: 'operator paused', iterations: { [gate]: 3 },
  2045	  1992	    });
  2046	  1993	
  2047	  1994	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'resume'], io);

  2052	  1999	    assert.ok((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.iterations[gate] >= 3, 'the gate cap counter survives resume — no refund');
  2053	  2000	    // J1a: resume must pin SUCCESSFUL continuation of the clean case, not merely
  2054	  2001	    // "not-unknown + writer respawns". The clean subtask completes: exit 0, the
  2055	  2002	    // run reaches DONE, and the journal records a RESUME event BEFORE completion.

  2057	  2004	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed pipeline reaches DONE');
  2058	  2005	    const journal = (io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal] ?? '')
  2059	  2006	      .split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  2060	  2007	    const resumeIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.RESUME);

  2062	  2009	    const advanceIdx = journal.findIndex((e) => e.type === LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE);
  2063	  2010	    assert.ok(advanceIdx > resumeIdx, 'the RESUME event precedes the phase-advance that completes the run');
  2064	  2011	  });

  2163	    14	// modules (spec/state/children/failover) over the real command machinery.

  2220	    71	  return {

  2236	    87	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
  2237	    88	    journalSeq: 0,
