  2239	    90	    dedupeRing: [],
  2240	    91	  };
  2241	    92	}
  2242	    93	
  2243	    94	/**
  2244	    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
  2245	    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
  2246	    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
  2247	    98	 * spawn spec.
  2248	    99	 */
  2249	   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
  2250	   101	  const queue = [...script];
  2251	   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  2252	   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
  2253	   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
  2254	   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
  2255	   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
  2256	   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
  2257	   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
  2258	   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
  2259	   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  2260	   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
  2261	   112	    }
  2262	   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
  2263	   114	  };
  2264	   115	  fn.calls = calls;
  2265	   116	  return fn;
  2266	   117	}
  2267	   118	
  2268	   119	function makeLoopRepo({ spec = loopSpec(), files = {}, runner, execFile, env = {} } = {}) {
  2269	   120	  const io = makeIo({
  2270	   121	    now: T0,
  2271	   122	    host: 'loop-host',
  2272	   123	    pid: 4242,
  2273	   124	    startTime: 111000,
  2274	   125	    fencingSeed: 1,
  2275	   126	    env,
  2276	   127	    files: {
  2277	   128	      [SIG_ABS]: SIG_CONTENT,
  2278	   129	      '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2),
  2279	   130	      ...(spec ? { '/repo/loop.json': JSON.stringify(spec, null, 2) + '\n' } : {}),
  2280	   131	      ...files,
  2281	   132	    },
  2282	   133	  });
  2283	   134	  if (execFile) io.execFile = execFile;
  2284	   135	  const fake = runner ?? fakeRunner(io, []);
  2285	   136	  io.superviseChild = fake;
  2286	   137	  io.__runner = fake;
  2287	   138	  return io;
  2288	   139	}
  2289	   140	
  2290	   141	const argsOf = (io, i) => (io.__runner.calls[i]?.args ?? []).map(String);
  2291	   142	const serialize = (io, i) => argsOf(io, i).join(' ');
  2292	   143	const valAfter = (arr, flag) => { const i = arr.indexOf(flag); return i >= 0 ? arr[i + 1] : undefined; };
  2293	   144	const loopFile = (io, name) => io.files()[`${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/${name}`];
  2294	   145	
  2295	   146	// ===========================================================================
  2296	   147	describe('loop run — preconditions', () => {
  2297	   148	  it('no loop.json -> usage error (exit 2) naming loop.json / loop init, ZERO children spawned', async () => {
  2298	   149	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: null }); // no loop.json seeded
  2299	   150	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2300	   151	    assert.equal(code, 2);
  2301	   152	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /loop\.json|loop init/i, 'the error points at the missing spec, not a generic unknown-subcommand');
  2302	   153	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned when preconditions fail');
  2303	   154	  });
  2304	   155	
  2305	   156	  it('an invalid spec (unknown role) -> exit 2 surfacing validateLoopSpec errors, ZERO children spawned', async () => {
  2306	   157	    // Config MISSING plan-reviewer -> the spec references an unknown role.
  2307	   158	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
  2308	   159	    delete cfg.roles['plan-reviewer'];
  2309	   160	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2) } });
  2310	   161	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2311	   162	    assert.equal(code, 2);
  2312	   163	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /unknown role 'plan-reviewer'/, 'the validateLoopSpec error is surfaced');
  2313	   164	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned for an invalid spec');
  2314	   165	  });
  2315	   166	});
  2316	   167	
  2317	   168	// ===========================================================================
  2318	   169	describe('loop run — supervisor run lock', () => {
  2319	   170	  it('a LIVE supervisor.lock refuses a second run (exit 1) naming the live pid', async () => {
  2320	   171	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ execFile: undefined });
  2321	   172	    // A different, ALIVE supervisor already holds the lock.
  2322	   173	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555;
  2323	   174	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
  2324	   175	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
  2325	   176	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'loop-other' }));
  2326	   177	
  2327	   178	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2328	   179	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live supervisor blocks a second run');
  2329	   180	    assert.match(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /55555/, 'the refusal names the live supervisor pid');
  2330	   181	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned while another supervisor is live');
  2331	   182	  });
  2332	   183	
  2333	   184	  it('a provably-DEAD supervisor.lock is recovered and the run proceeds', async () => {
  2334	   185	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  2335	   186	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2336	   187	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2337	   188	    // Default processAlive: only this io's own pid is alive; 999999 is dead.
  2338	   189	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
  2339	   190	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
  2340	   191	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'loop-dead' }));
  2341	   192	
  2342	   193	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2343	   194	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a dead lock is reclaimed and the run completes');
  2344	   195	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'children were spawned after recovering the dead lock');
  2345	   196	  });
  2346	   197	});
  2347	   198	
  2348	   199	// ===========================================================================
  2349	   200	describe('loop run — phase driving', () => {
  2350	   201	  it('a 2-phase run (plan -> gate-1), both APPROVED: children on the RESOLVED assignments, status done, state persisted', async () => {
  2351	   202	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  2352	   203	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2353	   204	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2354	   205	
  2355	   206	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2356	   207	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a fully-APPROVED run finishes clean');
  2357	   208	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'exactly one child per phase');
  2358	   209	
  2359	   210	    // Phase 1: planner -> claude-code (chain head), headless -p.
  2360	   211	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude');
  2361	   212	    assert.ok(argsOf(io, 0).includes('-p'), 'the planner child runs headless');
  2362	   213	    // Phase 2: plan-reviewer -> codex/gpt-5.6-sol, reviewer read-only sandbox.
  2363	   214	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'codex');
  2364	   215	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the reviewer child carries the resolved model');
  2365	   216	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '-s'), 'read-only', 'the reviewer runs in a read-only sandbox');
  2366	   217	
  2367	   218	    // Every spawn is given a real per-child log path under .handoff/loop/children/
  2368	   219	    // plus its platform and timeout/grace budget.
  2369	   220	    for (const [i] of io.__runner.calls.entries()) {
  2370	   221	      const opts = io.__runner.calls[i].opts;
  2371	   222	      assert.match(String(opts.logPath), /\/\.handoff\/loop\/children\//, `child ${i} log path is under .handoff/loop/children/`);
  2372	   223	      assert.ok(typeof opts.platform === 'string' && opts.platform.length > 0, `child ${i} opts carry the platform`);
  2373	   224	      assert.ok(typeof opts.timeoutMs === 'number' && opts.timeoutMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a timeout`);
  2374	   225	      assert.ok(typeof opts.graceMs === 'number' && opts.graceMs > 0, `child ${i} opts carry a grace period`);
  2375	   226	    }
  2376	   227	
  2377	   228	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  2378	   229	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached done');
  2379	   230	    assert.equal(state.phaseIndex, 2, 'both phases advanced');
  2380	   231	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'state.json'), 'loop state persisted under .handoff/loop');
  2381	   232	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(loopPaths('/repo').journal), 'the loop journal persisted');
  2382	   233	  });
  2383	   234	});
  2384	   235	
  2385	   236	// ===========================================================================
  2386	   237	describe('loop run — gate iteration + the 5-cap', () => {
  2387	   238	  const gateSpec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
  2388	   239	
  2389	   240	  it('BLOCKED re-runs with findings in the retry prompt; the 6th attempt NEVER spawns; escalated + ESCALATION.md; exit 3', async () => {
  2390	   241	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: gateSpec, runner: undefined });
  2391	   242	    // Six BLOCKED results queued; only five may ever be consumed.
  2392	   243	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  2393	   244	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'FINDING-ALPHA-1' },
  2394	   245	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'FINDING-BETA-2' },
  2395	   246	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f3' },
  2396	   247	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f4' },
  2397	   248	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f5' },
  2398	   249	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'f6' },
  2399	   250	    ]);
  2400	   251	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2401	   252	
  2402	   253	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2403	   254	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'escalation exits 3 (defined nonzero)');
  2404	   255	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 5, 'exactly five gate attempts — a 6th is never spawned');
  2405	   256	    // Each retry carries the PRECEDING attempt's findings — later findings are
  2406	   257	    // not dropped in favor of the first.
  2407	   258	    assert.match(serialize(io, 1), /FINDING-ALPHA-1/, "the 2nd attempt's prompt carries the 1st attempt's findings");
  2408	   259	    assert.match(serialize(io, 2), /FINDING-BETA-2/, "the 3rd attempt's prompt carries the 2nd attempt's findings (continuity, not just the first)");
  2409	   260	
  2410	   261	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  2411	   262	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the run escalated at the cap');
  2412	   263	    const esc = loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md');
  2413	   264	    assert.ok(esc, 'an ESCALATION.md was written under .handoff/loop');
  2414	   265	    assert.match(esc, /gate-1/, 'the escalation names the exhausted gate');
  2415	   266	  });
  2416	   267	});
  2417	   268	
  2418	   269	// ===========================================================================
  2419	   270	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
  2420	   271	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
  2421	   272	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
  2422	   273	    phases: [
  2423	   274	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
  2424	   275	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
  2425	   276	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
  2426	   277	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
  2427	   278	    ],
  2428	   279	  });
  2429	   280	
  2430	   281	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
  2431	   282	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
  2432	   283	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
  2433	   284	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
  2434	   285	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
  2435	   286	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
  2436	   287	    };
  2437	   288	  }
  2438	   289	
  2439	   290	  it('after smoke-build: runs smoke.cmd, writes SMOKE-REVIEW.md + a smoke-approval token, awaits, exit 0, no further child', async () => {
  2440	   291	    const rec = [];
  2441	   292	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
  2442	   293	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2443	   294	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2444	   295	
  2445	   296	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2446	   297	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'pausing for smoke approval is a clean exit 0');
  2447	   298	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 3, 'the smoke slice ran (3 phases); the post-smoke phase did NOT spawn');
  2448	   299	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
  2449	   300	
  2450	   301	    assert.ok(loopFile(io, 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md'), 'SMOKE-REVIEW.md was written');
  2451	   302	    const approval = loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json');
  2452	   303	    assert.ok(approval, 'a smoke-approval record was written');
  2453	   304	    const token = JSON.parse(approval).token;
  2454	   305	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.startsWith('smk1.'), 'the approval carries a smokeApprovalToken over the six inputs');
  2455	   306	
  2456	   307	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  2457	   308	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run is awaiting smoke approval');
  2458	   309	  });
  2459	   310	
  2460	   311	  it('--approve-smoke <correct token> resumes into the next phase (a child spawns again)', async () => {
  2461	   312	    const rec = [];
  2462	   313	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: smokeExecFile(rec), runner: undefined });
  2463	   314	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2464	   315	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2465	   316	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
  2466	   317	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
  2467	   318	
  2468	   319	    // Resume with the correct token — a FRESH runner for the continued phase.
  2469	   320	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2470	   321	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
  2471	   322	    io.__runner = runner2;
  2472	   323	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
  2473	   324	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  2474	   325	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 1, 'a verified approval continues into the post-smoke phase');
  2475	   326	  });
  2476	   327	
  2477	   328	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
  2478	   329	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
  2479	   330	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
  2480	   331	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
  2481	   332	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
  2482	   333	      if (cmd === 'git') {
  2483	   334	        const key = args.join(' ');
  2484	   335	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
  2485	   336	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
  2486	   337	      }
  2487	   338	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
  2488	   339	    };
  2489	   340	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
  2490	   341	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2491	   342	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2492	   343	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
  2493	   344	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
  2494	   345	
  2495	   346	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
  2496	   347	    head = 'HEAD-B';
  2497	   348	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2498	   349	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
  2499	   350	    io.__runner = runner2;
  2500	   351	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
  2501	   352	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
  2502	   353	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
  2503	   354	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  2504	   355	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
  2505	   356	  });
  2506	   357	});
  2507	   358	
  2508	   359	// ===========================================================================
  2509	   360	// D9 (v1.1 item 1) — done-status persistence. A smoke-approve resume that
  2510	   361	// completes the FINAL phase must persist raw status 'done'. Today the last
  2511	   362	// phase's PHASE_ADVANCE flips DONE, SMOKE_AWAIT overwrites it, and SMOKE_APPROVE
  2512	   363	// resumes to RUNNING — nothing re-flips DONE, so the completion path prints
  2513	   364	// done + exits 0 while state.json stays 'running'.
  2514	   365	describe('loop run — D9: done-status persistence', () => {
  2515	   366	  const smokeExec = (rec = []) => (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
  2516	   367	    rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
  2517	   368	    if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
  2518	   369	    return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
  2519	   370	  };
  2520	   371	  // smoke-build is the LAST phase — no phase after the smoke gate re-flips DONE.
  2521	   372	  const finalSmokeSpec = loopSpec({
  2522	   373	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
  2523	   374	    phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }, { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' }],
  2524	   375	  });
  2525	   376	
  2526	   377	  it('RED (D9): a smoke-approved run that completes its FINAL phase persists raw status done', async () => {
  2527	   378	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: finalSmokeSpec, execFile: smokeExec(), runner: undefined });
  2528	   379	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // work + smoke-build
  2529	   380	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2530	   381	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0, 'the first pass reaches the smoke gate');
  2531	   382	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token;
  2532	   383	
  2533	   384	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, []); // no phase remains after the smoke gate
  2534	   385	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
  2535	   386	    io.__runner = runner2;
  2536	   387	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io);
  2537	   388	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the approved completion exits 0');
  2538	   389	    const raw = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'state.json'));
  2539	   390	    assert.equal(raw.status, 'done', 'the completed run persists status done (not left running after SMOKE_APPROVE)');
  2540	   391	  });
  2541	   392	
  2542	   393	  it('GUARD (D9): re-running a completed (done) run still prints done and exits 0, no re-spawn', async () => {
  2543	   394	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] });
  2544	   395	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2545	   396	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, []);
  2546	   397	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2547	   398	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
  2548	   399	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
  2549	   400	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/state.json`, JSON.stringify({
  2550	   401	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-done', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 1, phaseIndex: 1,
  2551	   402	      iterations: {}, status: 'done', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  2552	   403	      flavor: 'loop', specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases),
  2553	   404	    }, null, 2) + '\n');
  2554	   405	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2555	   406	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a re-run of a done run exits 0');
  2556	   407	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /done/, 'it still prints done');
  2557	   408	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child re-spawns for a completed run');
  2558	   409	  });
  2559	   410	});
  2560	   411	
  2561	   412	// ===========================================================================
  2562	   413	// ITEM 3 (v1.1) — per-phase child budgets. Today budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase
  2563	   414	// gates a GLOBAL product (childSeq >= maxChildrenPerPhase * phases.length);
  2564	   415	// enforce it PER phase so one runaway phase parks naming itself while others are
  2565	   416	// unaffected, and the SUM across phases may legitimately exceed a single budget.
  2566	   417	describe('loop run — per-phase child budgets (item 3)', () => {
  2567	   418	  // A runner that ALWAYS returns BLOCKED — the phase never passes on its own.
  2568	   419	  const alwaysBlocked = (io) => {
  2569	   420	    const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  2570	   421	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
  2571	   422	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [] });
  2572	   423	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'still-not-done', logPath: opts.logPath });
  2573	   424	    };
  2574	   425	    fn.calls = calls;
  2575	   426	    return fn;
  2576	   427	  };
  2577	   428	
  2578	   429	  it('RED (item 3): a phase exhausting its OWN child budget parks naming that phase (global product NOT exhausted)', async () => {
  2579	   430	    // 3 phases → the GLOBAL product is 2*3 = 6, far from exhausted; gate-1 alone
  2580	   431	    // must park at its OWN budget of 2, before the 5-iteration cap trips.
  2581	   432	    const spec = loopSpec({
  2582	   433	      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
  2583	   434	      phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }, { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'gate-2', role: 'plan-reviewer' }],
  2584	   435	    });
  2585	   436	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2586	   437	    const runner = alwaysBlocked(io);
  2587	   438	    io.superviseChild = runner;
  2588	   439	    io.__runner = runner;
  2589	   440	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2590	   441	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'exhausting a single phase’s child budget parks (exit 4), not escalates at the cap');
  2591	   442	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  2592	   443	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
  2593	   444	    assert.match(state.parkReason ?? '', /gate-1/, 'the park reason names the phase whose budget was exhausted');
  2594	   445	    // The phase SPENT its full budget (not an immediate/early park): exactly 2
  2595	   446	    // children ran, each a BLOCKED gate iteration, so the budget — not something
  2596	   447	    // else — is the binding constraint.
  2597	   448	    assert.equal(runner.calls.length, 2, `the phase spent exactly its 2-child budget before parking; got ${runner.calls.length}`);
  2598	   449	    assert.equal(state.iterations['gate-1'], 2, 'both budgeted children ran their BLOCKED gate iterations (iterations[gate-1] === 2)');
  2599	   450	  });
  2600	   451	
  2601	   452	  it('GUARD (item 3): phases each UNDER their own budget complete even when the SUM exceeds one phase budget', async () => {
  2602	   453	    // 3 phases, budget 2 each. Each spends 2 children (BLOCKED then APPROVED) —
  2603	   454	    // total 6 across phases > any single budget (2), and > a naive global 2.
  2604	   455	    const spec = loopSpec({
  2605	   456	      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
  2606	   457	      phases: [{ id: 'p1', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p2', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p3', role: 'planner' }],
  2607	   458	    });
  2608	   459	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2609	   460	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  2610	   461	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p1: 2 children
  2611	   462	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p2: 2 children
  2612	   463	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p3: 2 children
  2613	   464	    ]);
  2614	   465	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2615	   466	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2616	   467	    assert.equal(code, 0, `no per-phase budget is exceeded, so the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2617	   468	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
  2618	   469	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 6, 'the SUM of children (6) exceeds any single phase budget (2) — no global-product regression');
  2619	   470	  });
  2620	   471	});
  2621	   472	
  2622	   473	// ===========================================================================
  2623	   474	// ITEM 4 (v1.1) — findings persistence. Append-only .handoff/loop/findings/
  2624	   475	// <gate>.ndjson, one line per BLOCKED verdict {iteration?, findings, at}; the
  2625	   476	// next child prompt for that gate embeds the LATEST persisted findings, and
  2626	   477	// escalations embed them too — so a park→resume or a crash→re-invoke re-prompts
  2627	   478	// with the real prior findings instead of an empty string.
  2628	   479	describe('loop run — findings persistence (item 4)', () => {
  2629	   480	  const P = loopPaths('/repo');
  2630	   481	  const findingsLines = (io, gate) => {
  2631	   482	    const raw = io.files()[`${P.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`];
  2632	   483	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  2633	   484	  };
  2634	   485	  const seedRunningState = (io, over) => {
  2635	   486	    io.fs.mkdirSync(P.dir, { recursive: true });
  2636	   487	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/state.json`, JSON.stringify({
  2637	   488	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-item4', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 1, phaseIndex: 0,
  2638	   489	      iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  2639	   490	      flavor: 'loop', ...over,
  2640	   491	    }, null, 2) + '\n');
  2641	   492	  };
  2642	   493	
  2643	   494	  it('RED (4a): a BLOCKED gate then PARK then resume carries the persisted findings into the next child prompt', async () => {
  2644	   495	    const marker = 'DISTINCTIVE_FINDINGS_4A_MARKER';
  2645	   496	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
  2646	   497	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh']; // single entry — a model death parks
  2647	   498	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
  2648	   499	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
  2649	   500	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  2650	   501	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: marker }, // records the gate findings
  2651	   502	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child died: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // model death → single-entry chain parks
  2652	   503	    ]);
  2653	   504	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2654	   505	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 4, 'run 1 parks after recording the BLOCKED findings');
  2655	   506	    assert.ok(findingsLines(io, 'gate-1').some((l) => String(l.findings).includes(marker)), 'the BLOCKED findings were persisted to findings/gate-1.ndjson');
  2656	   507	
  2657	   508	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2658	   509	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
  2659	   510	    io.__runner = runner2;
  2660	   511	    await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
  2661	   512	    assert.ok(runner2.calls.length >= 1, 'resume re-spawned the gate child');
  2662	   513	    assert.match((runner2.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' '), new RegExp(marker), 'the resumed child prompt embeds the persisted prior findings');
  2663	   514	  });
  2664	   515	
  2665	   516	  // Two ordered persisted lines — the LATEST must win, the older must not be
  2666	   517	  // selected as the active prior findings.
  2667	   518	  const OLD = 'OLDER_FINDINGS_SUPERSEDED';
  2668	   519	  const NEW = 'NEWER_FINDINGS_LATEST';
  2669	   520	  const seedTwoFindings = (io, gate) => {
  2670	   521	    io.fs.mkdirSync(`${P.dir}/findings`, { recursive: true });
  2671	   522	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`,
  2672	   523	      JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: OLD, at: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ iteration: 2, findings: NEW, at: T0 }) + '\n');
  2673	   524	  };
  2674	   525	
  2675	   526	  it('RED (4b loop): a re-invoke embeds the LATEST persisted findings in the FIRST child prompt (older superseded)', async () => {
  2676	   527	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
  2677	   528	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2678	   529	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2679	   530	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2680	   531	    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 2 } });
  2681	   532	    seedTwoFindings(io, 'gate-1');
  2682	   533	
  2683	   534	    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2684	   535	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'a child spawned on re-invoke');
  2685	   536	    const prompt = (io.__runner.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' ');
  2686	   537	    assert.match(prompt, new RegExp(NEW), 'the first re-invoke child prompt embeds the LATEST persisted findings');
  2687	   538	    assert.doesNotMatch(prompt, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not selected as the active prior findings');
  2688	   539	  });
  2689	   540	
  2690	   541	  it('RED (4c loop): a fresh-invoke cap escalation embeds the LATEST persisted findings in ESCALATION.md (older superseded)', async () => {
  2691	   542	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
  2692	   543	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2693	   544	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // unused — escalates before spawn
  2694	   545	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2695	   546	    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 1 } }); // AT cap 1
  2696	   547	    seedTwoFindings(io, 'gate-1');
  2697	   548	
  2698	   549	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2699	   550	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a gate at the cap escalates');
  2700	   551	    const esc = loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md') ?? '';
  2701	   552	    assert.match(esc, new RegExp(NEW), 'ESCALATION.md embeds the LATEST persisted findings even when in-memory findings are empty');
  2702	   553	    assert.doesNotMatch(esc, new RegExp(OLD), 'the superseded (older) findings is not the one embedded');
  2703	   554	  });
  2704	   555	
  2705	   556	  it('RED (4d): the findings file is append-only across iterations (two BLOCKED verdicts → two ordered lines)', async () => {
  2706	   557	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
  2707	   558	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2708	   559	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  2709	   560	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'first-finding-A' },
  2710	   561	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'second-finding-B' },
  2711	   562	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' },
  2712	   563	    ]);
  2713	   564	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2714	   565	    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2715	   566	    const lines = findingsLines(io, 'gate-1');
  2716	   567	    assert.equal(lines.length, 2, 'two BLOCKED verdicts append two findings lines (append-only, never overwrite)');
  2717	   568	    assert.match(String(lines[0].findings), /first-finding-A/, 'the first BLOCKED findings is line 1');
  2718	   569	    assert.match(String(lines[1].findings), /second-finding-B/, 'the second BLOCKED findings is line 2 (order preserved)');
  2719	   570	  });
  2720	   571	});
  2721	   572	
  2722	   573	// ===========================================================================
  2723	   574	// ITEM 4b (v1.1) — review artifacts from gate children. After EVERY verdict
  2724	   575	// parse for a gate child the supervisor writes reviews/<runId>/<gate>/
  2725	   576	// iteration-NN/{prompt.md, verdict.md} in the repo tree (layout per
  2726	   577	// reviews/README.md): prompt.md is the child prompt verbatim; verdict.md is a
  2727	   578	// header (role/model/date) + the parsed verdict + findings. NN = the gate
  2728	   579	// iteration count at parse time (01-based). Writes are FAIL-OPEN.
  2729	   580	describe('loop run — review artifacts from gate children (item 4b)', () => {
  2730	   581	  const promptOf = (call) => (call?.args ?? []).map(String).find((a) => a.includes('You are the')) ?? '';
  2731	   582	  // Parse a verdict.md into its leading `key: value` header block + trailing
  2732	   583	  // body (findings), per reviews/README.md's header convention.
  2733	   584	  const parseVerdictMd = (text) => {
  2734	   585	    const lines = String(text ?? '').split('\n');
  2735	   586	    const header = /** @type {Record<string,string>} */ ({});
  2736	   587	    let i = 0;
  2737	   588	    for (; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  2738	   589	      const m = lines[i].match(/^([A-Za-z][\w-]*):\s?(.*)$/);
  2739	   590	      if (!m) break;
  2740	   591	      header[m[1].toLowerCase()] = m[2].trim();
  2741	   592	    }
  2742	   593	    return { header, body: lines.slice(i).join('\n').trim() };
  2743	   594	  };
  2744	   595	
  2745	   596	  it('RED (4b-1): a two-iteration gate leaves iteration-01/02 with verbatim prompt.md + full-contract verdict.md', async () => {
  2746	   597	    const marker = 'FIX_THE_THING_MARKER';
  2747	   598	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
  2748	   599	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2749	   600	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  2750	   601	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: marker },
  2751	   602	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' },
  2752	   603	    ]);
  2753	   604	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2754	   605	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2755	   606	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the gate completes after the APPROVED; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2756	   607	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  2757	   608	    const base = `/repo/reviews/${runId}/gate-1`;
  2758	   609	    const f = (rel) => io.files()[`${base}/${rel}`];
  2759	   610	
  2760	   611	    // iteration-01 — the BLOCKED attempt (01-based iteration count at parse time).
  2761	   612	    assert.ok(f('iteration-01/prompt.md'), 'iteration-01/prompt.md written');
  2762	   613	    assert.ok(f('iteration-01/verdict.md'), 'iteration-01/verdict.md written');
  2763	   614	    // prompt.md is EXACTLY the child prompt, verbatim.
  2764	   615	    assert.equal(f('iteration-01/prompt.md'), promptOf(io.__runner.calls[0]), 'iteration-01 prompt.md === the child prompt verbatim');
  2765	   616	    // verdict.md carries the FULL header contract + parsed verdict + findings.
  2766	   617	    const v1 = parseVerdictMd(f('iteration-01/verdict.md'));
  2767	   618	    assert.equal(v1.header.role, 'plan-reviewer', 'verdict header: exact role');
  2768	   619	    assert.match(v1.header.model ?? '', /gpt-5\.6-sol/, 'verdict header: concrete model');
  2769	   620	    assert.match(v1.header.model ?? '', /xhigh/, 'verdict header: the effort actually used');
  2770	   621	    assert.match(`${v1.header.harness ?? ''}${v1.header.platform ?? ''}`, /codex/i, 'verdict header: harness/platform');
  2771	   622	    assert.equal(v1.header.date, '2026-07-19', 'verdict header: date (YYYY-MM-DD from io.now())');
  2772	   623	    assert.equal(v1.header.verdict, 'BLOCKED', 'verdict header: exact verdict');
  2773	   624	    assert.ok('degraded' in v1.header, 'verdict header: degraded field present');
  2774	   625	    assert.equal(v1.body, marker, 'verdict.md body is the exact findings text');
  2775	   626	
  2776	   627	    // iteration-02 — the APPROVED attempt; its prompt embeds the prior findings.
  2777	   628	    assert.ok(f('iteration-02/prompt.md'), 'iteration-02/prompt.md written');
  2778	   629	    assert.equal(f('iteration-02/prompt.md'), promptOf(io.__runner.calls[1]), 'iteration-02 prompt.md === the second child prompt verbatim');
  2779	   630	    assert.match(f('iteration-02/prompt.md'), new RegExp(marker), 'the second prompt embeds the prior findings');
  2780	   631	    const v2 = parseVerdictMd(f('iteration-02/verdict.md'));
  2781	   632	    assert.equal(v2.header.role, 'plan-reviewer', 'iteration-02 verdict header: exact role');
  2782	   633	    assert.match(v2.header.model ?? '', /gpt-5\.6-sol/, 'iteration-02 verdict header: concrete model');
  2783	   634	    assert.match(v2.header.model ?? '', /xhigh/, 'iteration-02 verdict header: the effort actually used');
  2784	   635	    assert.match(`${v2.header.harness ?? ''}${v2.header.platform ?? ''}`, /codex/i, 'iteration-02 verdict header: harness/platform');
  2785	   636	    assert.equal(v2.header.date, '2026-07-19', 'iteration-02 verdict header: exact date');
  2786	   637	    assert.equal(v2.header.verdict, 'APPROVED', 'iteration-02 verdict header: exact verdict');
  2787	   638	    assert.ok('degraded' in v2.header, 'iteration-02 verdict header: degraded field present');
  2788	   639	    assert.equal(v2.body, '', 'iteration-02 verdict.md body is the exact findings (empty for a clean APPROVED)');
  2789	   640	  });
  2790	   641	
  2791	   642	  it('RED (4b-2, fail-open): an artifact write error WARNS but never fails the run', async () => {
  2792	   643	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
  2793	   644	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
  2794	   645	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2795	   646	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2796	   647	    // Every write under reviews/ throws — the run must not be failed by it.
  2797	   648	    const realWrite = io.fs.writeFileSync.bind(io.fs);
  2798	   649	    io.fs.writeFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ pth, /** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {any} */ o) => {
  2799	   650	      if (String(pth).includes('/reviews/')) throw Object.assign(new Error('EACCES: reviews/ not writable'), { code: 'EACCES' });
  2800	   651	      return realWrite(pth, data, o);
  2801	   652	    };
  2802	   653	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2803	   654	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'an artifact write error does NOT fail the run (fail-open)');
  2804	   655	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run still completed');
  2805	   656	    const runId = (await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.runId;
  2806	   657	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), new RegExp(`reviews/${runId}/gate-1/iteration-01/(prompt|verdict)\\.md`), 'the warning names the CONCRETE failed artifact path');
  2807	   658	  });
  2808	   659	
  2809	   660	  it('GUARD (4b): a DEAD (failover) gate child writes NO verdict.md — only parsed verdicts leave artifacts', async () => {
  2810	   661	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
  2811	   662	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh']; // single entry — a model death parks
  2812	   663	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
  2813	   664	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
  2814	   665	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child died: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }]);
  2815	   666	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2816	   667	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2817	   668	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the model-unavailable death parks');
  2818	   669	    const verdictArtifacts = Object.keys(io.files()).filter((k) => /\/reviews\/.*\/verdict\.md$/.test(k));
  2819	   670	    assert.deepEqual(verdictArtifacts, [], 'a child that produced no parsed verdict leaves no verdict.md');
  2820	   671	  });
  2821	   672	});
  2822	   673	
  2823	   674	// ===========================================================================
  2824	   675	// ITEM 8 (v1.1) — probe integration. Each invocation loads the doctor's cached
  2825	   676	// availability probes ONCE (readProbeCache, 15-min freshness) and passes that
  2826	   677	// snapshot as probes: into EVERY resolveRoles/runFailover call. A fresh cache
  2827	   678	// marking a platform rate-limited diverts resolution off it; a stale/missing
  2828	   679	// cache is null → today's offline-degraded behavior, byte-identical.
  2829	   680	describe('loop run — probe integration (item 8)', () => {
  2830	   681	  const CACHE = '/repo/.handoff/log/probe-cache.json';
  2831	   682	  const seedCache = (io, records, at = T0) => {
  2832	   683	    io.fs.mkdirSync('/repo/.handoff/log', { recursive: true });
  2833	   684	    io.fs.writeFileSync(CACHE, JSON.stringify({ at, records }));
  2834	   685	  };
  2835	   686	  const soloImpl = () => loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] });
  2836	   687	
  2837	   688	  it('RED (8-1): a fresh cache rate-limiting the head platform diverts the FIRST spawn to the fallback', async () => {
  2838	   689	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
  2839	   690	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2840	   691	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2841	   692	    // implementer chain head is claude-code; rate-limit it → codex fallback.
  2842	   693	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }]);
  2843	   694	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2844	   695	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes on the fallback; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2845	   696	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 1, 'one child spawned');
  2846	   697	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'codex', 'the first spawn diverted to codex — the rate-limited claude-code never spawned');
  2847	   698	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 0), '--model'), 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'the resolved model is the fallback chain entry');
  2848	   699	  });
  2849	   700	
  2850	   701	  it('RED (8-1 failover): a loop failover relaunch honors the cache — dead head avoided, rate-limited middle skipped for the surviving tail', async () => {
  2851	   702	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
  2852	   703	    cfg.roles['trio'] = ['codex/cx-head@xhigh', 'claude-code/cc-mid', 'cursor/cu-tail'];
  2853	   704	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'trio' }] });
  2854	   705	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2) }, runner: undefined });
  2855	   706	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
  2856	   707	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // cx-head dies model-unavailable
  2857	   708	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // the relaunched child
  2858	   709	    ]);
  2859	   710	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2860	   711	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }]);
  2861	   712	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2862	   713	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the relaunch completes on the surviving tail; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2863	   714	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 2, 'the head died and exactly one relaunch spawned');
  2864	   715	    assert.notEqual(io.__runner.calls[1].command, 'claude', 'the failover skipped the rate-limited claude-code middle entry');
  2865	   716	    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(io, 1), '--model'), 'cu-tail', 'the relaunch landed on the surviving cursor tail (cache reached loop runFailover)');
  2866	   717	  });
  2867	   718	
  2868	   719	  it('GUARD (8-4): a STALE cache and a MISSING cache both change nothing — the head spawns as today', async () => {
  2869	   720	    const staleAt = new Date(Date.parse(T0) - 20 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); // 20 min old, past the window
  2870	   721	    const ioStale = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
  2871	   722	    ioStale.superviseChild = fakeRunner(ioStale, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2872	   723	    ioStale.__runner = ioStale.superviseChild;
  2873	   724	    seedCache(ioStale, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'rate-limited' }], staleAt);
  2874	   725	    await cmdLoop(['run'], ioStale);
  2875	   726	    assert.equal(ioStale.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'a stale cache is ignored — the head (claude-code) spawns as today');
  2876	   727	
  2877	   728	    const ioNone = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
  2878	   729	    ioNone.superviseChild = fakeRunner(ioNone, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2879	   730	    ioNone.__runner = ioNone.superviseChild;
  2880	   731	    await cmdLoop(['run'], ioNone);
  2881	   732	    assert.equal(ioNone.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'no cache file — the head spawns as today');
  2882	   733	  });
  2883	   734	
  2884	   735	  it('GUARD (8-5): a fresh cache marking the head merely unverifiable (installed/ok) still resolves it — degraded, never blocked', async () => {
  2885	   736	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: soloImpl(), runner: undefined });
  2886	   737	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2887	   738	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2888	   739	    seedCache(io, [{ platform: 'claude-code', capability: 'installed', outcome: 'ok' }]);
  2889	   740	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2890	   741	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'an unverifiable-but-not-rate-limited head is not blocked');
  2891	   742	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls[0].command, 'claude', 'installed/ok is selectable (degraded) — the head still spawns, never skipped');
  2892	   743	  });
  2893	   744	});
  2894	   745	
  2895	   746	// ===========================================================================
  2896	   747	// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach. `baton loop run --detach` forks a background
  2897	   748	// supervisor that survives the invoking terminal; the parent returns promptly
  2898	   749	// once the detached child owns the run lock, printing its pid + a tail hint.
  2899	   750	//
  2900	   751	// SEAM JUDGMENT CALL (flagged for the verifier): a REAL fork is not honestly
  2901	   752	// unit-testable (an orphaned process, timing), so these pin the behavior through
  2902	   753	// an injected `io.spawnDetached(spec) -> { pid }` seam the implementation should
  2903	   754	// honor (default: a real detached child_process.spawn). Assumptions pinned:
  2904	   755	//   - the PARENT checks the run lock (refuse-live / reclaim-dead) BEFORE forking;
  2905	   756	//   - the DETACHED CHILD owns the lock (the fake writes it naming the child pid);
  2906	   757	//   - spawnDetached receives the capped supervisor.out output path.
  2907	   758	// Windows: --detach is POSIX-only (documented unsupported) — SKIP_WIN.
  2908	   759	describe('loop run — --detach (item 10)', () => {
  2909	   760	  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
  2910	   761	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  2911	   762	  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
  2912	   763	    const rec = { calls: [] };
  2913	   764	    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
  2914	   765	      rec.calls.push(spec);
  2915	   766	      if (writeLock) {
  2916	   767	        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  2917	   768	        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
  2918	   769	      }
  2919	   770	      return { pid };
  2920	   771	    };
  2921	   772	    return rec;
  2922	   773	  };
  2923	   774	
  2924	   775	  it('RED (10-1): --detach forks a detached supervisor; the parent exits 0 after the child owns the lock, printing pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  2925	   776	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  2926	   777	    const DETACHED_PID = 424242;
  2927	   778	    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
  2928	   779	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  2929	   780	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2930	   781	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
  2931	   782	    const lock = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]);
  2932	   783	    assert.equal(lock.pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
  2933	   784	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
  2934	   785	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
  2935	   786	  });
  2936	   787	
  2937	   788	  it('RED (10-2): --detach refuses (exit 1) when a LIVE supervisor already holds the lock — no fork', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  2938	   789	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  2939	   790	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 55555;
  2940	   791	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 1);
  2941	   792	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  2942	   793	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'live' }));
  2943	   794	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  2944	   795	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live lock refuses the detached run (exit 1)');
  2945	   796	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 0, 'no detached supervisor is forked while the lock is held live');
  2946	   797	  });
  2947	   798	
  2948	   799	  it('RED (10-3): --detach reclaims a DEAD lock, killing recorded in-flight child groups BEFORE forking', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  2949	   800	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  2950	   801	    const kills = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  2951	   802	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { kills.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
  2952	   803	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 9001; // orphan group alive; lock pid 999999 dead
  2953	   804	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
  2954	   805	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  2955	   806	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
  2956	   807	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  2957	   808	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  2958	   809	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming detached run starts; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2959	   810	    assert.ok(kills.some((k) => k.pid === -9001), `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; kills: ${JSON.stringify(kills)}`);
  2960	   811	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'after reclaiming, the detached supervisor is forked');
  2961	   812	  });
  2962	   813	
  2963	   814	  it('GUARD (10-4): a completing run releases the lock — the release contract a detached supervisor reuses', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  2964	   815	    // The detached child runs the same supervisor body; isolating its release
  2965	   816	    // needs a real child, so this pins the reused contract on the attached path.
  2966	   817	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] }), runner: undefined });
  2967	   818	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  2968	   819	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  2969	   820	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  2970	   821	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  2971	   822	    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'a completing run releases the supervisor.lock');
  2972	   823	  });
  2973	   824	
  2974	   825	  it('RED (10-5): --detach directs the detached output to a byte-capped .handoff/loop/supervisor.out', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  2975	   826	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  2976	   827	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
  2977	   828	    await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  2978	   829	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the detach seam was invoked');
  2979	   830	    const spec = seam.calls[0];
  2980	   831	    assert.match(String(spec.outPath ?? spec.out ?? ''), /\.handoff\/loop\/supervisor\.out$/, 'the detached output is directed to .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
  2981	   832	    const cap = spec.maxBytes ?? spec.cap ?? spec.capBytes;
  2982	   833	    assert.ok(typeof cap === 'number' && cap > 0, `a positive byte cap is passed for supervisor.out; got ${JSON.stringify(cap)}`);
  2983	   834	  });
  2984	   835	});
  2985	   836

  3138	   989	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE);

  3153	  1004	
  3154	  1005	  it('--approve-smoke with no value is a usage error (exit 2) naming the flag + value, NOT "unknown flag"', async () => {

  3156	  1007	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke'], io);
  3157	  1008	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a valueless --approve-smoke must not silently run unapproved');
  3158	  1009	    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
  3159	  1010	    assert.match(out, /--approve-smoke/, 'the diagnostic names the flag');
  3160	  1011	    assert.match(out, /value/i, 'the diagnostic says a value is required');
  3161	  1012	    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /unknown flag/i, '--approve-smoke joins the strict spec — it is a KNOWN flag missing its value');
  3162	  1013	  });
  3163	  1014	
  3164	  1015	  it('BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD does NOT suppress loop run (supervisor-side)', async () => {
  3165	  1016	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ env: { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1' }, runner: undefined });
  3166	  1017	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  3167	  1018	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  3168	  1019	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3169	  1020	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'the guard is child-only; the supervisor command still runs');
  3170	  1021	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'children were spawned under the guard env');
  3171	  1022	  });
  3172	  1023	});
  3173	  1024	
  3174	  1025	// ===========================================================================
  3175	  1026	// B8 (G2) — the run-lock acquisition must be ATOMIC, not check-then-write.
  3176	  1027	describe('loop run — atomic run-lock acquisition (B8)', () => {
  3177	  1028	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
  3178	  1029	  const LOCK = `${DIR}/supervisor.lock`;
  3179	  1030	
  3180	  1031	  it('RED (B8): implementation-neutral — the competitor materializes AT the exclusive-create; wx/mkdir-first refuses, check-then-write clobbers (RED)', async () => {
  3181	  1032	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3182	  1033	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  3183	  1034	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  3184	  1035	    io.processAlive = (p) => p === 55555; // the competitor supervisor is LIVE
  3185	  1036	
  3186	  1037	    const realWrite = io.fs.writeFileSync.bind(io.fs);
  3187	  1038	    const realMkdir = io.fs.mkdirSync.bind(io.fs);
  3188	  1039	    const realRename = io.fs.renameSync.bind(io.fs);
  3189	  1040	    const COMPETITOR = JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'competitor' });
  3190	  1041	
  3191	  1042	    // The competitor wins the lock the INSTANT before the supervisor's own write
  3192	  1043	    // to LOCK lands — injected at whichever primitive the acquire uses to create
  3193	  1044	    // it. NO readFileSync hook, so a wx/mkdir-FIRST acquire (which never reads
  3194	  1045	    // before creating) cannot dodge the race.
  3195	  1046	    let injected = false;
  3196	  1047	    const injectBefore = (/** @type {any} */ target) => {
  3197	  1048	      if (injected || String(target) !== LOCK) return;
  3198	  1049	      injected = true;
  3199	  1050	      realMkdir(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3200	  1051	      realWrite(LOCK, COMPETITOR);
  3201	  1052	    };
  3202	  1053	
  3203	  1054	    // Exclusive create (flag wx/ax) enforces the O_EXCL semantics memfs lacks:
  3204	  1055	    // once the competitor exists, an exclusive write throws EEXIST.
  3205	  1056	    io.fs.writeFileSync = (/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
  3206	  1057	      const flag = typeof opts === 'string' ? undefined : opts?.flag;
  3207	  1058	      const exclusive = flag === 'wx' || flag === 'ax' || flag === 'wx+' || flag === 'ax+';
  3208	  1059	      injectBefore(p);
  3209	  1060	      if (exclusive && String(p) === LOCK && io.fs.existsSync(p)) throw Object.assign(new Error(`EEXIST: file already exists, open '${p}'`), { code: 'EEXIST' });
  3210	  1061	      return realWrite(p, data, opts); // a PLAIN write to LOCK clobbers the competitor
  3211	  1062	    };
  3212	  1063	    // mkdirSync(LOCK) as an exclusive lock dir: memfs already throws EEXIST when a
  3213	  1064	    // node exists at the path (the injected competitor file).
  3214	  1065	    io.fs.mkdirSync = (/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {any} */ opts) => { injectBefore(p); return realMkdir(p, opts); };
  3215	  1066	    // A tmp+rename publish (atomicWriteJson) clobbers the competitor on rename.
  3216	  1067	    io.fs.renameSync = (/** @type {any} */ from, /** @type {any} */ to) => { injectBefore(to); return realRename(from, to); };
  3217	  1068	
  3218	  1069	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3219	  1070	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a wx/mkdir-first exclusive acquire observes EEXIST and refuses the run');
  3220	  1071	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 0, 'no child spawned when the lock was lost to the competitor');
  3221	  1072	    assert.match(JSON.parse(io.files()[LOCK]).runId, /competitor/, "the competitor's lock is not clobbered (a check-then-write path fails here)");
  3222	  1073	  });
  3223	  1074	});
  3224	  1075	
  3225	  1076	// ===========================================================================
  3226	  1077	// G4 (A4) — record in-flight child groups; kill recorded orphans on reclaim.
  3227	  1078	describe('loop run — child-group recording + kill-on-reclaim (G4)', () => {
  3228	  1079	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
  3229	  1080	
  3230	  1081	  const readChildren = (io) => {
  3231	  1082	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`];
  3232	  1083	    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return [];
  3233	  1084	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  3234	  1085	  };
  3235	  1086	
  3236	  1087	  it('RED (G4): a child-start record {childId, pid, pgid, startedAt} exists on disk WHILE the child is in flight (before completion)', async () => {
  3237	  1088	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3238	  1089	    let n = 0;
  3239	  1090	    const inflightOk = [];
  3240	  1091	    // The runner reports the child's group via opts.onStart (spawn time); the
  3241	  1092	    // supervisor must persist the record synchronously in that handler — BEFORE
  3242	  1093	    // the child completes — so a mid-run crash leaves the orphan reap-able.
  3243	  1094	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
  3244	  1095	      n += 1;
  3245	  1096	      const info = { pid: 8000 + n, pgid: 9000 + n };
  3246	  1097	      if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function') opts.onStart(info);
  3247	  1098	      const rec = readChildren(io).find((r) => r.pgid === info.pgid);
  3248	  1099	      inflightOk.push(
  3249	  1100	        !!rec && typeof rec.childId === 'string' && rec.childId.length > 0 && rec.pid === info.pid && typeof rec.startedAt === 'string' && rec.startedAt.length > 0,
  3250	  1101	      );
  3251	  1102	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath, ...info });
  3252	  1103	    };
  3253	  1104	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3254	  1105	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  3255	  1106	    assert.ok(inflightOk.length >= 1, 'at least one child ran');
  3256	  1107	    assert.ok(inflightOk.every(Boolean), 'each child-start record ({childId,pid,pgid,startedAt}) existed on disk while its child was in flight');
  3257	  1108	  });
  3258	  1109	
  3259	  1110	  it('RED (G5/G4): on dead-lock reclaim, a still-alive recorded orphan group is KILLED (io.processKill) BEFORE the first new spawn', async () => {
  3260	  1111	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3261	  1112	    const events = []; // ordered: kills and spawns interleaved
  3262	  1113	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { events.push({ kind: 'kill', pid, sig }); return true; };
  3263	  1114	    const baseRunner = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  3264	  1115	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => { events.push({ kind: 'spawn' }); return baseRunner(spec, opts); };
  3265	  1116	    io.__runner = baseRunner;
  3266	  1117	    // The dead supervisor's orphan group (pgid 9001) is still alive; its lock is dead.
  3267	  1118	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9001;
  3268	  1119	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3269	  1120	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
  3270	  1121	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  3271	  1122	
  3272	  1123	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3273	  1124	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes after reaping orphans; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  3274	  1125	    const firstSpawn = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'spawn');
  3275	  1126	    const killIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'kill' && Math.abs(e.pid) === 9001);
  3276	  1127	    assert.ok(killIdx >= 0, `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
  3277	  1128	    assert.ok(firstSpawn === -1 || killIdx < firstSpawn, 'the orphan group was killed BEFORE the first new child spawned');
  3278	  1129	  });
  3279	  1130	});

  3368	  1219	// H3 (A2/B5) — children.ndjson RETIRES completed children; reclaim kills only
  3369	  1220	// still-in-flight groups (never an OS-recycled pid of a retired child).
  3370	  1221	describe('loop run — child registry retirement (H3)', () => {
  3371	  1222	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
  3372	  1223	  const readChildren = (io) => {
  3373	  1224	    const raw = io.files()[`${DIR}/children.ndjson`];
  3374	  1225	    if (typeof raw !== 'string') return [];
  3375	  1226	    return raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
  3376	  1227	  };
  3377	  1228	
  3378	  1229	  it('RED (H3a): after a clean run, EVERY started child has a matching retire record ({childId, endedAt})', async () => {
  3379	  1230	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3380	  1231	    // A runner that reports its group (so the supervisor writes the START record
  3381	  1232	    // via its onStart handler); the RETIRE record is the supervisor's job on resolve.
  3382	  1233	    let n = 0;
  3383	  1234	    const notRetiredAtStart = [];
  3384	  1235	    io.superviseChild = (/** @type {any} */ _spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
  3385	  1236	      n += 1;
  3386	  1237	      const pgid = 9000 + n;
  3387	  1238	      if (typeof opts.onStart === 'function') opts.onStart({ pid: 8000 + n, pgid });
  3388	  1239	      // Ordering: the child is IN FLIGHT here — it must have a start record and
  3389	  1240	      // NO retire yet (retirement happens only on resolution).
  3390	  1241	      const start = readChildren(io).find((r) => r.pgid === pgid);
  3391	  1242	      const retiredEarly = readChildren(io).some((r) => start && r.childId === start.childId && typeof r.endedAt === 'string');
  3392	  1243	      notRetiredAtStart.push(!!start && !retiredEarly);
  3393	  1244	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath, pgid });
  3394	  1245	    };
  3395	  1246	    io.__runner = { calls: [] };
  3396	  1247	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3397	  1248	    assert.ok(notRetiredAtStart.length >= 1 && notRetiredAtStart.every(Boolean), 'a just-started child has a start record and NO endedAt while in flight');
  3398	  1249	    assert.equal(code, 0);
  3399	  1250	    const recs = readChildren(io);
  3400	  1251	    const started = recs.filter((r) => typeof r.pgid === 'number').map((r) => r.childId);
  3401	  1252	    const retired = recs.filter((r) => typeof r.endedAt === 'string').map((r) => r.childId);
  3402	  1253	    assert.ok(started.length >= 1, 'at least one child started');
  3403	  1254	    for (const id of started) assert.ok(retired.includes(id), `child ${id} was retired (a {childId, endedAt} record exists)`);
  3404	  1255	  });
  3405	  1256	
  3406	  1257	  it('RED (H3b): a dead-lock reclaim KILLS NOTHING for a RETIRED child, even when processAlive says its (recycled) pgid is alive', async () => {
  3407	  1258	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3408	  1259	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);

  3412	  1263	    // The prior run's child is RETIRED, but its pgid was recycled by an unrelated live process.
  3413	  1264	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ p) => p === 9001;
  3414	  1265	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3415	  1266	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
  3416	  1267	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
  3417	  1268	      `${DIR}/children.ndjson`,
  3418	  1269	      JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n' + JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', endedAt: T0 }) + '\n',
  3419	  1270	    );
  3420	  1271	
  3421	  1272	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3422	  1273	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  3423	  1274	    assert.ok(!killCalls.some((k) => Math.abs(k.pid) === 9001), `a retired child is never killed (pid-reuse safety); kills: ${JSON.stringify(killCalls)}`);
  3424	  1275	  });
  3425	  1276	});

  3427	  1278	// ===========================================================================
  3428	  1279	// H5 (B4) vice-versa — a pipeline-flavored state is refused by `loop run`.
  3429	  1280	describe('loop run — state flavor binding (H5)', () => {
  3430	  1281	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
  3431	  1282	  it('RED (H5): a state.json created by `pipeline run` (flavor pipeline) is REFUSED by loop run (exit 2), zero spawns', async () => {
  3432	  1283	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3433	  1284	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  3434	  1285	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  3435	  1286	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3436	  1287	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
  3437	  1288	      `${DIR}/state.json`,
  3438	  1289	      JSON.stringify({
  3439	  1290	        schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-seeded', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 2, phaseIndex: 0,
  3440	  1291	        iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
  3441	  1292	        flavor: 'pipeline',

  3443	  1294	    );

  3549	  1400	    );

  3601	  1452	// I5 (B5) — the run lock passes startTime to processAlive: a recycled pid
  3602	  1453	// (alive by pid alone but failing the pid+startTime pair) is reclaimed, not
  3603	  1454	// refused as live.
  3604	  1455	describe('loop run — lock reclaim uses pid+startTime (I5)', () => {
  3605	  1456	  const DIR = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}`;
  3606	  1457	  it('RED (I5): a lock whose pid is alive by a 1-arg check but fails the (pid, startTime) pair is treated as DEAD and reclaimed', async () => {
  3607	  1458	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3608	  1459	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  3609	  1460	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  3610	  1461	    // pid 7777 reads as alive for ANY defined startTime EXCEPT the recorded 999
  3611	  1462	    // (which is the reused-pid case → dead). A 1-arg check, or a wrong-but-defined
  3612	  1463	    // second arg, would read live and wedge the lock. Only passing the RECORDED
  3613	  1464	    // startTime (999) reclaims — so the fake is inverted vs. a "match one value"
  3614	  1465	    // trap, and the calls are recorded to prove the pair is passed through.
  3615	  1466	    const aliveCalls = [];
  3616	  1467	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {number | undefined} */ startTime) => {
  3617	  1468	      aliveCalls.push({ pid, startTime });
  3618	  1469	      if (pid !== 7777) return false;
  3619	  1470	      return startTime !== 999; // dead ONLY for the recorded (pid, startTime) pair
  3620	  1471	    };
  3621	  1472	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3622	  1473	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 7777, startTime: 999, runId: 'recycled' }));
  3623	  1474	
  3624	  1475	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
  3625	  1476	    assert.equal(code, 0, `a reused-pid lock is reclaimed (not refused as live); stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  3626	  1477	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'the run proceeds after reclaiming the reused-pid lock');
  3627	  1478	    assert.ok(
  3628	  1479	      aliveCalls.some((c) => c.pid === 7777 && c.startTime === 999),
  3629	  1480	      `the reclaim passes the RECORDED (pid, startTime) pair to processAlive; calls: ${JSON.stringify(aliveCalls)}`,
  3630	  1481	    );
  3631	  1482	  });

  3855	+// ===========================================================================
  3856	+// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach. `baton loop run --detach` forks a background
  3857	+// supervisor that survives the invoking terminal; the parent returns promptly
  3858	+// once the detached child owns the run lock, printing its pid + a tail hint.
  3859	+//
  3860	+// SEAM JUDGMENT CALL (flagged for the verifier): a REAL fork is not honestly
  3861	+// unit-testable (an orphaned process, timing), so these pin the behavior through
  3862	+// an injected `io.spawnDetached(spec) -> { pid }` seam the implementation should
  3863	+// honor (default: a real detached child_process.spawn). Assumptions pinned:
  3864	+//   - the PARENT checks the run lock (refuse-live / reclaim-dead) BEFORE forking;
  3865	+//   - the DETACHED CHILD owns the lock (the fake writes it naming the child pid);
  3866	+//   - spawnDetached receives the capped supervisor.out output path.
  3867	+// Windows: --detach is POSIX-only (documented unsupported) — SKIP_WIN.
  3868	+describe('loop run — --detach (item 10)', () => {
  3869	+  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
  3870	+  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  3871	+  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
  3872	+    const rec = { calls: [] };
  3873	+    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
  3874	+      rec.calls.push(spec);
  3875	+      if (writeLock) {
  3876	+        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3877	+        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
  3878	+      }
  3879	+      return { pid };
  3880	+    };
  3881	+    return rec;
  3882	+  };
  3883	+
  3884	+  it('RED (10-1): --detach forks a detached supervisor; the parent exits 0 after the child owns the lock, printing pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  3885	+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3886	+    const DETACHED_PID = 424242;
  3887	+    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
  3888	+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  3889	+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  3890	+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
  3891	+    const lock = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]);
  3892	+    assert.equal(lock.pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
  3893	+    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
  3894	+    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
  3895	+  });
  3896	+
  3897	+  it('RED (10-2): --detach refuses (exit 1) when a LIVE supervisor already holds the lock — no fork', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  3898	+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3899	+    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 55555;
  3900	+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 1);
  3901	+    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3902	+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'live' }));
  3903	+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  3904	+    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live lock refuses the detached run (exit 1)');
  3905	+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 0, 'no detached supervisor is forked while the lock is held live');
  3906	+  });
  3907	+
  3908	+  it('RED (10-3): --detach reclaims a DEAD lock, killing recorded in-flight child groups BEFORE forking', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  3909	+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3910	+    const kills = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  3911	+    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { kills.push({ pid, sig }); return true; };
  3912	+    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 9001; // orphan group alive; lock pid 999999 dead
  3913	+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
  3914	+    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  3915	+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
  3916	+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
  3917	+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  3918	+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming detached run starts; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  3919	+    assert.ok(kills.some((k) => k.pid === -9001), `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; kills: ${JSON.stringify(kills)}`);
  3920	+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'after reclaiming, the detached supervisor is forked');
  3921	+  });
  3922	+
  3923	+  it('GUARD (10-4): a completing run releases the lock — the release contract a detached supervisor reuses', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  3924	+    // The detached child runs the same supervisor body; isolating its release
  3925	+    // needs a real child, so this pins the reused contract on the attached path.
  3926	+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] }), runner: undefined });
  3927	+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  3928	+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;

  3930	+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  3931	+    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'a completing run releases the supervisor.lock');
  3932	+  });
  3933	+
  3934	+  it('RED (10-5): --detach directs the detached output to a byte-capped .handoff/loop/supervisor.out', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  3935	+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  3936	+    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
  3937	+    await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  3938	+    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the detach seam was invoked');
  3939	+    const spec = seam.calls[0];
  3940	+    assert.match(String(spec.outPath ?? spec.out ?? ''), /\.handoff\/loop\/supervisor\.out$/, 'the detached output is directed to .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
  3941	+    const cap = spec.maxBytes ?? spec.cap ?? spec.capBytes;
  3942	+    assert.ok(typeof cap === 'number' && cap > 0, `a positive byte cap is passed for supervisor.out; got ${JSON.stringify(cap)}`);
  3943	+  });
  3944	+});

  3959	+// pipeline whose loop.json sets smoke.cmd reuses the loop's smoke machinery
  3960	+// (smokeApprovalToken/verifySmokeToken, SMOKE_AWAIT/SMOKE_APPROVE, --approve-smoke):
