  3962	+describe('pipeline — smoke gate (item 9)', () => {
  3963	+  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  3964	+  // A git that also answers the smoke command (non-git → 'ok'), so merges work
  3965	+  // AND the smoke.cmd runs through io.execFile.
  3966	+  const smokeAwareIo = (spec, { git = pipelineGit() } = {}) => {
  3967	+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec, git, runner: undefined });
  3968	+    const baseExec = io.execFile;
  3969	+    io.execFile = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) =>
  3970	+      cmd === 'git' ? baseExec(cmd, args, opts) : Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });

  3976	+
  3977	+  it('RED (9-1): a pipeline with smoke.cmd AWAITS approval after the last subtask merges (not straight to DONE)', async () => {

  3981	+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL, 'the run awaits smoke approval, not DONE');

  3989	+

  4080	   746	// ===========================================================================
  4081	   747	// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach. `baton loop run --detach` forks a background
  4082	   748	// supervisor that survives the invoking terminal; the parent returns promptly
  4083	   749	// once the detached child owns the run lock, printing its pid + a tail hint.
  4084	   750	//
  4085	   751	// SEAM JUDGMENT CALL (flagged for the verifier): a REAL fork is not honestly
  4086	   752	// unit-testable (an orphaned process, timing), so these pin the behavior through
  4087	   753	// an injected `io.spawnDetached(spec) -> { pid }` seam the implementation should
  4088	   754	// honor (default: a real detached child_process.spawn). Assumptions pinned:
  4089	   755	//   - the PARENT checks the run lock (refuse-live / reclaim-dead) BEFORE forking;
  4090	   756	//   - the DETACHED CHILD owns the lock (the fake writes it naming the child pid);
  4091	   757	//   - spawnDetached receives the capped supervisor.out output path.
  4092	   758	// Windows: --detach is POSIX-only (documented unsupported) — SKIP_WIN.
  4093	   759	describe('loop run — --detach (item 10)', () => {
  4094	   760	  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
  4095	   761	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  4096	   762	  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
  4097	   763	    const rec = { calls: [] };
  4098	   764	    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
  4099	   765	      rec.calls.push(spec);
  4100	   766	      if (writeLock) {
  4101	   767	        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
  4102	   768	        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
  4103	   769	      }
  4104	   770	      return { pid };
  4105	   771	    };
  4106	   772	    return rec;
  4107	   773	  };
  4108	   774	
  4109	   775	  it('RED (10-1): --detach forks a detached supervisor; the parent exits 0 after the child owns the lock, printing pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
  4110	   776	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
  4111	   777	    const DETACHED_PID = 424242;
  4112	   778	    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
  4113	   779	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
  4114	   780	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
  4115	   781	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
  4116	   782	    const lock = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]);
  4117	   783	    assert.equal(lock.pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
  4118	   784	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
  4119	   785	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
  4120	   786	  });
  4121	   787

  4302	   281	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
  4303	   282	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
  4304	   283	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
  4305	   284	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
  4306	   285	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
  4307	   286	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
  4308	   287	    };
  4309	   288	  }

  4349	   328	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
  4350	   329	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
  4351	   330	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
  4352	   331	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
  4353	   332	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
  4354	   333	      if (cmd === 'git') {
  4355	   334	        const key = args.join(' ');
  4356	   335	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
  4357	   336	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
  4358	   337	      }
  4359	   338	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
  4360	   339	    };
  4361	   340	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
  4362	   341	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  4363	   342	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  4364	   343	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
  4365	   344	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
  4366	   345	
  4367	   346	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
  4368	   347	    head = 'HEAD-B';
  4369	   348	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
  4370	   349	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
  4371	   350	    io.__runner = runner2;
  4372	   351	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
  4373	   352	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
  4374	   353	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
  4375	   354	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  4376	   355	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
  4377	   356	  });
  4378	   357	});

  4970	     7	export function newBundleId(rand = Math.random) {
  4971	     8	  const a = rand().toString(36).slice(2);
  4972	     9	  const b = rand().toString(36).slice(2);
  4973	    10	  return `b_${(a + b).slice(0, 16) || '0'}`;

  4976	    13	/**
  4977	    14	 * Canonical JSON: object keys sorted ascending at every depth; array order
  4978	    15	 * preserved (order is meaningful there).
  4979	    16	 * @param {any} value @returns {string}
  4980	    17	 */
  4981	    18	function canonicalJson(value) {
  4982	    19	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
  4983	    20	    return `[${value.map(canonicalJson).join(',')}]`;
  4984	    21	  }
  4985	    22	  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
  4986	    23	    const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();
  4987	    24	    return `{${keys.map((k) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonicalJson(value[k])}`).join(',')}}`;
  4988	    25	  }
  4989	    26	  return JSON.stringify(value);
  4990	    27	}
  4991	    28	
  4992	    29	/**
  4993	    30	 * sha256 hex over canonical JSON — the journal's idempotency key.
  4994	    31	 * @param {unknown} obj @returns {string}
  4995	    32	 */
  4996	    33	export function dedupeKey(obj) {
  4997	    34	  return createHash('sha256').update(canonicalJson(obj)).digest('hex');

  5017	    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
  5018	    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
  5019	    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
  5020	    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
  5021	    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
  5022	    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
  5023	    21	  DONE: 'done',
  5024	    22	});
  5025	    23	
  5026	    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
  5027	    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
  5028	    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
  5029	    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
  5030	    28	  ESCALATE: 'escalate',
  5031	    29	  PARK: 'park',
  5032	    30	  RESUME: 'resume',
  5033	    31	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
  5034	    32	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
  5035	    33	});
  5036	    34	
  5037	    35	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
  5038	    36	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
  5039	    37	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
  5040	    38	
  5041	    39	/** @param {string} root */
  5042	    40	export function loopPaths(root) {
  5043	    41	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
  5044	    42	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
  5045	    43	}
  5046	    44	
  5047	    45	/**
  5048	    46	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
  5049	    47	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
  5050	    48	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
  5051	    49	 */
  5052	    50	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
  5053	    51	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
  5054	    52	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
  5055	    53	  const iterations = {};
  5056	    54	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
  5057	    55	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
  5058	    56	  }
  5059	    57	  return {
  5060	    58	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
  5061	    59	    runId,
  5062	    60	    goal: spec.goal,
  5063	    61	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
  5064	    62	    phaseIndex: 0,
  5065	    63	    iterations,
  5066	    64	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
  5067	    65	    parkReason: null,
  5068	    66	    escalation: null,
  5069	    67	    smokeApproval: null,
  5070	    68	    createdAt: io.now(),
  5071	    69	    journalSeq: 0,
  5072	    70	  };
  5073	    71	}
  5074	    72	
  5075	    73	/**
  5076	    74	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
  5077	    75	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
  5078	    76	 * only produced for a real transition).
  5079	    77	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
  5080	    78	 */
  5081	    79	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
  5082	    80	  switch (ev?.type) {
  5083	    81	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
  5084	    82	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
  5085	    83	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
  5086	    84	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
  5087	    85	    }
  5088	    86	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
  5089	    87	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
  5090	    88	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
  5091	    89	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
  5092	    90	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
  5093	    91	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
  5094	    92	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
  5095	    93	      }
  5096	    94	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
  5097	    95	    }
  5098	    96	    case LOOP_EVENT.ESCALATE: {
  5099	    97	      // Supervisors enforce spec caps LOWER than the hard invariant; this
  5100	    98	      // event persists that escalation (dogfood finding D7: a cap-3 pipeline
  5101	    99	      // exit left state.json 'running' because only the reducer's own 5-cap
  5102	   100	      // could flip the status). Idempotent — an already-escalated run keeps
  5103	   101	      // its original record.
  5104	   102	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) return state;
  5105	   103	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
  5106	   104	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0 } };
  5107	   105	    }
  5108	   106	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
  5109	   107	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
  5110	   108	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
  5111	   109	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
  5112	   110	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
  5113	   111	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
  5114	   112	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
  5115	   113	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
  5116	   114	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
  5117	   115	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
  5118	   116	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
  5119	   117	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
  5120	   118	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
  5121	   119	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
  5122	   120	      // A smoke gate on the FINAL phase already advanced past phaseCount —
  5123	   121	      // approval completes the run as DONE, not RUNNING (dogfood finding D9:
  5124	   122	      // the printed done/exit 0 left a 'running' artifact behind).
  5125	   123	      const resumedStatus = state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount ? LOOP_STATUS.DONE : LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING;
  5126	   124	      return { ...state, status: resumedStatus, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
  5127	   125	    }
  5128	   126	    default:
  5129	   127	      return state;
  5130	   128	  }
  5131	   129	}

  5137	   135	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
  5138	
  5139	exec
  5140	/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/commands/loop.mjs | sed -n '119,226p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
  5141	 succeeded in 0ms:
  5142	   119	 * child groups (.handoff/loop/children.ndjson) are KILLED first so no
  5143	   120	 * orphan survives the takeover, then the lock is retried once.
  5144	   121	 * Shared by `loop run` and `pipeline run`.
  5145	   122	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} cmdLabel
  5146	   123	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, release: () => void} | {ok: false, code: number}>}
  5147	   124	 */
  5148	   125	export async function acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, cmdLabel) {
  5149	   126	  const p = loopPaths(root);
  5150	   127	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
  5151	   128	  const childrenPath = `${p.dir}/children.ndjson`;
  5152	   129	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  5153	   130	  const payload = JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId: `sup-${io.pid}` });
  5154	   131	
  5155	   132	  const tryExclusive = () => {
  5156	   133	    // Fast-path an already-present lock (fakes without O_EXCL semantics rely
  5157	   134	    // on this); the wx flag below is the REAL exclusivity on a POSIX fs — a
  5158	   135	    // competitor that lands between the check and the write throws EEXIST
  5159	   136	    // instead of being clobbered.
  5160	   137	    if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) return false;
  5161	   138	    try {
  5162	   139	      io.fs.writeFileSync(lockPath, payload, { flag: 'wx' });
  5163	   140	      return true;
  5164	   141	    } catch (err) {
  5165	   142	      if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') return false;
  5166	   143	      throw err;
  5167	   144	    }
  5168	   145	  };
  5169	   146	
  5170	   147	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt += 1) {
  5171	   148	    if (tryExclusive()) {
  5172	   149	      // A fresh acquisition owns a fresh child registry: prior invocations'
  5173	   150	      // start/retire records are truncated so a stale retire can never mask a
  5174	   151	      // later in-flight child that reuses the same childId (I2).
  5175	   152	      try {
  5176	   153	        if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
  5177	   154	      } catch {
  5178	   155	        // Best effort.
  5179	   156	      }
  5180	   157	      return {
  5181	   158	        ok: true,
  5182	   159	        release: () => {
  5183	   160	          try {
  5184	   161	            if (io.fs.existsSync(lockPath)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
  5185	   162	          } catch {
  5186	   163	            // Best effort — a stale lock is recoverable via the dead path.
  5187	   164	          }
  5188	   165	        },
  5189	   166	      };
  5190	   167	    }
  5191	   168	    const existing = safeReadJson(io.fs, lockPath);
  5192	   169	    const other = existing.ok ? existing.value : null;
  5193	   170	    // Liveness verifies the (pid, startTime) PAIR — a recycled pid without the
  5194	   171	    // recorded start time is a dead owner, not a live supervisor (I5).
  5195	   172	    const alive = other && typeof other.pid === 'number' && typeof io.processAlive === 'function' ? io.processAlive(other.pid, other.startTime) : true;
  5196	   173	    const sameProcess = other && other.pid === io.pid && other.startTime === io.startTime;
  5197	   174	    if (!sameProcess && (!other || other.host !== io.host || alive)) {
  5198	   175	      io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: another supervisor (pid ${other?.pid ?? 'unknown'} on ${other?.host ?? 'unknown'}, run ${other?.runId ?? '?'}) holds the run lock — refusing a second supervisor\n`);
  5199	   176	      return { ok: false, code: 1 };
  5200	   177	    }
  5201	   178	    // Provably dead (or our own stale lock): reap its recorded in-flight
  5202	   179	    // child groups BEFORE anything else runs, then reclaim.
  5203	   180	    if (io.fs.existsSync(childrenPath)) {
  5204	   181	      const lines = String(io.fs.readFileSync(childrenPath, 'utf8')).split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '');
  5205	   182	      // Match retires to starts IN ORDER per childId — a resumed invocation
  5206	   183	      // reuses childIds, and a prior run's retire must never mask the newer
  5207	   184	      // in-flight start of the same id (I2). Whatever start remains unmatched
  5208	   185	      // is genuinely in-flight.
  5209	   186	      /** @type {Map<string, any[]>} */
  5210	   187	      const inflight = new Map();
  5211	   188	      for (const line of lines) {
  5212	   189	        try {
  5213	   190	          const rec = JSON.parse(line);
  5214	   191	          if (typeof rec?.endedAt === 'string') inflight.get(rec.childId)?.shift();
  5215	   192	          else if (typeof rec?.pgid === 'number') {
  5216	   193	            if (!inflight.has(rec.childId)) inflight.set(rec.childId, []);
  5217	   194	            inflight.get(rec.childId)?.push(rec);
  5218	   195	          }
  5219	   196	        } catch {
  5220	   197	          // A torn record is unreapable — skip it.
  5221	   198	        }
  5222	   199	      }
  5223	   200	      // Kill ONLY genuinely in-flight children (start with no matching
  5224	   201	      // retire): a retired child's pgid may have been recycled by an
  5225	   202	      // unrelated live process — killing it would be a pid-reuse casualty (H3).
  5226	   203	      for (const recs of inflight.values()) {
  5227	   204	        for (const rec of recs) {
  5228	   205	          if (typeof io.processAlive === 'function' && io.processAlive(rec.pgid)) {
  5229	   206	            if (typeof io.processKill === 'function') io.processKill(-rec.pgid, 'SIGKILL');
  5230	   207	          }
  5231	   208	        }
  5232	   209	      }
  5233	   210	      try {
  5234	   211	        io.fs.unlinkSync(childrenPath);
  5235	   212	      } catch {
  5236	   213	        // Best effort.
  5237	   214	      }
  5238	   215	    }
  5239	   216	    io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: reclaimed the run lock from a provably-dead supervisor (pid ${other?.pid})\n`);
  5240	   217	    try {
  5241	   218	      io.fs.unlinkSync(lockPath);
  5242	   219	    } catch {
  5243	   220	      // Already gone.
  5244	   221	    }
  5245	   222	  }
  5246	   223	  io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: could not acquire the run lock after reclaiming — another supervisor keeps winning\n`);
  5247	   224	  return { ok: false, code: 1 };
  5248	   225	}

  5264	   228	/**
  5265	   229	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
  5266	   230	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
  5267	   231	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
  5268	   232	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
  5269	   233	 * @returns {string}
  5270	   234	 */
  5271	   235	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
  5272	   236	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
  5273	   237	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
  5274	   238	  }
  5275	   239	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
  5276	   240	}
  5277	   241	
  5278	   242	/**
  5279	   243	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
  5280	   244	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
  5281	   245	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
  5282	   246	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
  5283	   247	 */
  5284	   248	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
  5285	   249	  /** @type {string} */
  5286	   250	  let current;
  5287	   251	  try {
  5288	   252	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
  5289	   253	  } catch {
  5290	   254	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
  5291	   255	  }
  5292	   256	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
  5293	   257	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
  5294	   258	  const b = current.split('.');
  5295	   259	  /** @type {string[]} */
  5296	   260	  const drifted = [];
  5297	   261	  SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.forEach((f, i) => {
  5298	   262	    if (a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]) drifted.push(f);
  5299	   263	  });
  5300	   264	  return { ok: false, driftedInputs: drifted.length > 0 ? drifted : [...SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS] };
  5301	   265	}

  5594	    77	/**
  5595	    78	 * Derive the repo's trunk branch EXACTLY ONCE per run: origin's HEAD when it
  5596	    79	 * exists, else the currently checked-out branch at the root. The result is
  5597	    80	 * stamped into state and reused on every resume — never re-derived mid-run.
  5598	    81	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  5599	    82	 * @returns {Promise<string>}
  5600	    83	 */
  5601	    84	async function deriveTrunk(root, io) {
  5602	    85	  try {
  5603	    86	    const out = String((await io.execFile('git', ['symbolic-ref', 'refs/remotes/origin/HEAD'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
  5604	    87	    const m = out.match(/refs\/remotes\/origin\/(.+)$/);
  5605	    88	    if (m?.[1]) return m[1];
  5606	    89	  } catch {
  5607	    90	    // No origin HEAD (local-only repo) — fall back to the checked-out branch.
  5608	    91	  }
  5609	    92	  const branch = String((await io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
  5610	    93	  return branch.length > 0 && branch !== 'HEAD' ? branch : 'main';
  5611	    94	}

  5613	    96	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
  5614	    97	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
  5615	    98	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
  5616	    99	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
  5617	   100	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
  5618	   101	  }
  5619	   102	  const seen = new Set();
  5620	   103	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
  5621	   104	    const st = subtasks[i];
  5622	   105	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
  5623	   106	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
  5624	   107	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
  5625	   108	    seen.add(st.id);
  5626	   109	  }
  5627	   110	  return null;
  5628	   111	}

  5642	     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
  5643	     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
  5644	     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
  5645	     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
  5646	     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
  5647	     6	//
  5648	     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
  5649	     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
  5650	     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
  5651	    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
  5652	    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
  5653	    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
  5654	    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
  5655	    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
  5656	    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
  5657	    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
  5658	    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
  5659	    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
  5660	    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
  5661	    20	
  5662	    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
  5663	    22	
  5664	    23	export function makeIo({
  5665	    24	  files = {},
  5666	    25	  env = {},
  5667	    26	  stdin = '',
  5668	    27	  execResults = {},
  5669	    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
  5670	    29	  host = 'host-A',
  5671	    30	  pid = 4242,
  5672	    31	  startTime = 111000,
  5673	    32	  processAlive = null,
  5674	    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
  5675	    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
  5676	    35	} = {}) {
  5677	    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
  5678	    37	  const outChunks = [];
  5679	    38	  const errChunks = [];
  5680	    39	  let currentNow = now;
  5681	    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
  5682	    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
  5683	    42	
  5684	    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  5685	    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  5686	    45	
  5687	    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
  5688	    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
  5689	    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  5690	    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
  5691	    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
  5692	    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
  5693	    52	        return reject(e);
  5694	    53	      }
  5695	    54	      const r = execResults[key];
  5696	    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
  5697	    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
  5698	    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
  5699	    58	      if (code !== 0) {
  5700	    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
  5701	    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
  5702	    61	        e.code = code;
  5703	    62	        e.stdout = out;
  5704	    63	        e.stderr = err;
  5705	    64	        return reject(e);
  5706	    65	      }
  5707	    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
  5708	    67	    });
  5709	    68	  };
  5710	    69	
  5711	    70	  return {
  5712	    71	    cwd: '/repo',
  5713	    72	    env,
  5714	    73	    stdin,
  5715	    74	    stdout,
  5716	    75	    stderr,
  5717	    76	    fs,
  5718	    77	    execFile,
  5719	    78	    now: () => currentNow,
  5720	    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
  5721	    80	    execPath,
  5722	    81	    host,
  5723	    82	    pid,
  5724	    83	    startTime,
  5725	    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
  5726	    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
  5727	    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
  5728	    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
  5729	    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
  5730	    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
  5731	    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
  5732	    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
  5733	    92	  };
  5734	    93	}

  6301	   139	### Supervisor lifetime and recovery (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 5)
  6302	   140	
  6303	   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
  6304	   142	
  6305	   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
  6306	   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
  6307	   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
  6308	   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
  6309	   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
  6310	   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
  6311	   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
  6312	   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
  6313	   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
  6314	   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
  6315	   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
  6316	   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
  6317	   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
  6318	   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  6319	
  6320	exec
  6321	/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba docs/design/core.md | sed -n '1,14p'" in /Users/saksham/baton

  6323	     1	# Core engine design — module APIs and test lists
  6324	     2	
  6325	     3	Authoritative plan: `docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md` (Gate-1 approved). Where this doc and the plan differ, **the plan wins** — the Concurrency, Receive-transaction, Journal-rotation, Lock-model, and Detector sections were amended across Gate-1 iterations 1–5 and are specified there in full.
  6326	     4	
  6327	     5	## Language and tooling
  6328	     6	
  6329	     7	Plain ESM JavaScript (`.mjs`) with JSDoc types, checked by `tsc --noEmit --checkJs` (dev-only). No build step: hooks invoke source directly. Zero runtime dependencies; strict spec-based flag parsing (`parseFlagsStrict` in commands/shared.mjs — unknown flags, stray positionals, and missing string-flag values are exit-2 usage errors, so a garbled safety flag can never silently lose intent); `node:test` with a small golden-file helper (`UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 npm test` regenerates). Node >= 20.
  6330	     8	
  6331	     9	Testability backbone: every command's `run()` takes an injected `io` object — `{ cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }`. Pure logic (reducers, renderers, resolvers, classifiers) takes plain data and returns plain data. Unit tests run over an in-memory fs fake (`tests/helpers/memfs.mjs`); a thin integration layer uses `fs.mkdtemp` on the real fs.

  6343	   165	 * zombie grandchildren). Output streams to a capped log at opts.logPath; the

  6348	   170	 */

  6350	   172	  return new Promise((resolve) => {

  6355	   177	      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],

  6357	   179	    });
  6358	   180	
  6359	   181	    // Report the group at spawn time so the supervisor can persist it BEFORE
  6360	   182	    // the child completes — a supervisor crash leaves the orphan reap-able.

   743	  });
   744	});

   746	// ===========================================================================
   747	// ITEM 10 (v1.1) — --detach. `baton loop run --detach` forks a background
   748	// supervisor that survives the invoking terminal; the parent returns promptly
   749	// once the detached child owns the run lock, printing its pid + a tail hint.
   750	//
   751	// SEAM JUDGMENT CALL (flagged for the verifier): a REAL fork is not honestly
   752	// unit-testable (an orphaned process, timing), so these pin the behavior through
   753	// an injected `io.spawnDetached(spec) -> { pid }` seam the implementation should
   754	// honor (default: a real detached child_process.spawn). Assumptions pinned:
   755	//   - the PARENT checks the run lock (refuse-live / reclaim-dead) BEFORE forking;
   756	//   - the DETACHED CHILD owns the lock (the fake writes it naming the child pid);
   757	//   - spawnDetached receives the capped supervisor.out output path.
   758	// Windows: --detach is POSIX-only (documented unsupported) — SKIP_WIN.
   759	describe('loop run — --detach (item 10)', () => {
   760	  const SKIP_WIN = process.platform === 'win32';
   761	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
   762	  const detachSeam = (io, pid, { writeLock = true } = {}) => {
   763	    const rec = { calls: [] };
   764	    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
   765	      rec.calls.push(spec);
   766	      if (writeLock) {
   767	        io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   768	        io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid, startTime: 1, runId: `sup-${pid}` }));
   769	      }
   770	      return { pid };
   771	    };
   772	    return rec;
   773	  };
   774	
   775	  it('RED (10-1): --detach forks a detached supervisor; the parent exits 0 after the child owns the lock, printing pid + tail hint', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   776	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   777	    const DETACHED_PID = 424242;
   778	    const seam = detachSeam(io, DETACHED_PID);
   779	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   780	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the parent returns promptly with exit 0; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   781	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the supervisor was forked via the detach seam exactly once');
   782	    const lock = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`]);
   783	    assert.equal(lock.pid, DETACHED_PID, 'the run lock names the detached supervisor pid');
   784	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), new RegExp(String(DETACHED_PID)), 'stdout prints the detached pid');
   785	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /supervisor\.out/, 'stdout prints a tail hint for .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
   786	  });
   787	
   788	  it('RED (10-2): --detach refuses (exit 1) when a LIVE supervisor already holds the lock — no fork', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   789	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   790	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 55555;
   791	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 1);
   792	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   793	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 55555, startTime: 222, runId: 'live' }));
   794	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   795	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'a live lock refuses the detached run (exit 1)');
   796	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 0, 'no detached supervisor is forked while the lock is held live');
   797	  });
   798	
   799	  it('RED (10-3): --detach reclaims a DEAD lock, killing recorded in-flight child groups BEFORE forking', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   800	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   801	    // ONE shared ordered event log — the -9001 kill must precede the fork.
   802	    const events = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   803	    io.processKill = (/** @type {number} */ pid, /** @type {any} */ sig) => { events.push({ kind: 'kill', pid, sig }); return true; };
   804	    io.processAlive = (/** @type {number} */ pid) => pid === 9001; // orphan group alive; lock pid 999999 dead
   805	    io.spawnDetached = (/** @type {any} */ spec) => {
   806	      events.push({ kind: 'fork', spec });
   807	      io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: 7, startTime: 1, runId: 'sup-7' }));
   808	      return { pid: 7 };
   809	    };
   810	    io.fs.mkdirSync(DIR, { recursive: true });
   811	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: 'loop-host', pid: 999999, startTime: 7, runId: 'crashed' }));
   812	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/children.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ childId: '001-plan', pid: 8001, pgid: 9001, startedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
   813	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   814	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the reclaiming detached run starts; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   815	    const killIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'kill' && e.pid === -9001);
   816	    const forkIdx = events.findIndex((e) => e.kind === 'fork');
   817	    assert.ok(killIdx >= 0, `the orphan group 9001 was killed on reclaim; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
   818	    assert.ok(forkIdx >= 0, 'the detached supervisor was forked after reclaiming');
   819	    assert.ok(killIdx < forkIdx, `the orphan kill (-9001) precedes the fork; events: ${JSON.stringify(events)}`);
   820	  });
   821	
   822	  it('GUARD (10-4): a completing run releases the lock — the release contract a detached supervisor reuses', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   823	    // The detached child runs the same supervisor body; isolating its release
   824	    // needs a real child, so this pins the reused contract on the attached path.
   825	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'work', role: 'implementer' }] }), runner: undefined });
   826	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   827	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   828	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   829	    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   830	    assert.ok(!io.fs.existsSync(`${DIR}/supervisor.lock`), 'a completing run releases the supervisor.lock');
   831	  });
   832	
   833	  it('RED (10-5): --detach directs the detached output to a byte-capped .handoff/loop/supervisor.out', { skip: SKIP_WIN }, async () => {
   834	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ runner: undefined });
   835	    const seam = detachSeam(io, 7);
   836	    await cmdLoop(['run', '--detach'], io);
   837	    assert.equal(seam.calls.length, 1, 'the detach seam was invoked');
   838	    const spec = seam.calls[0];
   839	    assert.match(String(spec.outPath ?? ''), /\.handoff\/loop\/supervisor\.out$/, 'spec.outPath directs the detached output to .handoff/loop/supervisor.out');
   840	    assert.ok(typeof spec.maxBytes === 'number' && spec.maxBytes > 0, `spec.maxBytes is a positive byte cap for supervisor.out; got ${JSON.stringify(spec.maxBytes)}`);
   841	  });
   842	});

  1330	  const DIR = loopPaths('/repo').dir;
  1331	  // A git that also answers the smoke command (non-git → 'ok'), so merges work
  1332	  // AND the smoke.cmd runs through io.execFile. Records every non-git (smoke)
  1333	  // exec with a snapshot of the merge receipts AT that moment, so a test can

    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    98	 * spawn spec.
    99	 */
   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   101	  const queue = [...script];
   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   112	    }
   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   114	  };
   115	  fn.calls = calls;
   116	  return fn;
   117	}

   270	describe('loop run — smoke gate', () => {
   271	  const smokeSpec = loopSpec({
   272	    smoke: { cmd: 'npm run smoke', expect: 'ok' },
   273	    phases: [
   274	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
   275	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },

   277	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },

   280	
   281	  function smokeExecFile(rec) {
   282	    return (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   283	      rec.push([cmd, ...args].join(' '));
   284	      // git stays unavailable; the smoke command "passes".
   285	      if (cmd === 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   286	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' });
   287	    };
   288	  }
   289

   294	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   295

   299	    assert.ok(rec.some((c) => /smoke/.test(c)), 'smoke.cmd was executed via io.execFile');
   300

   328	  it('--approve-smoke with the REAL issued token after a bound input drifted -> refused: exit 4, PARKED, zero spawns', async () => {
   329	    // A git-aware execFile whose HEAD is a mutable ref — HEAD is a bound token
   330	    // input, so moving it between issuance and approval is genuine drift.
   331	    let head = 'HEAD-A';
   332	    const gitAware = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = []) => {
   333	      if (cmd === 'git') {
   334	        const key = args.join(' ');
   335	        const out = key === 'rev-parse HEAD' ? head : key === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' ? 'main' : '';
   336	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: out + '\n', stderr: '' });
   337	      }
   338	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'ok\n', stderr: '' }); // the smoke command
   339	    };
   340	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: smokeSpec, execFile: gitAware, runner: undefined });
   341	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   342	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   343	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 0);
   344	    const token = JSON.parse(loopFile(io, 'smoke-approval.json')).token; // the ACTUAL issued smk1 token
   345	
   346	    // Drift a bound input: HEAD moves after the human looked.
   347	    head = 'HEAD-B';
   348	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   349	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   350	    io.__runner = runner2;
   351	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run', '--approve-smoke', token], io); // the OLD token, now stale
   352	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'the plan parks on smoke-token drift');
   353	    assert.equal(runner2.calls.length, 0, 'a drifted approval spawns no child');
   354	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   355	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'drift parks the run (not left silently awaiting)');
   356	  });

   215	// token's driftedInputs discipline).

   218	const SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX = 'smk1';
   219	const SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS = /** @type {const} */ ([
   220	  'stateDigest',
   221	  'smokeCmd',
   222	  'smokeOutputDigest',
   223	  'gitHead',
   224	  'gitContentDigest',
   225	  'childAssignment',
   226	]);
   227	
   228	/**
   229	 * Deterministic structured token. Per-field digests keep field boundaries
   230	 * (no concatenation collisions) and let verification name drifted inputs.
   231	 * Missing bound fields throw — a token over partial inputs is meaningless.
   232	 * @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   233	 * @returns {string}
   234	 */
   235	export function smokeApprovalToken(inputs) {
   236	  for (const f of SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS) {
   237	    if (typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') throw new Error(`smoke approval token: required input '${f}' is missing`);
   238	  }
   239	  return `${SMOKE_TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.map((f) => dedupeKey(inputs[f]).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
   240	}
   241	
   242	/**
   243	 * Recompute against current inputs; positional comparison names every
   244	 * drifted input. Never throws — partial current inputs simply cannot verify.
   245	 * @param {string} presented @param {Record<string, string>} inputs
   246	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, driftedInputs: string[]}}
   247	 */
   248	export function verifySmokeToken(presented, inputs) {
   249	  /** @type {string} */
   250	  let current;
   251	  try {
   252	    current = smokeApprovalToken(inputs);
   253	  } catch {
   254	    return { ok: false, driftedInputs: SMOKE_TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((f) => typeof inputs?.[f] !== 'string') };
   255	  }
   256	  if (presented === current) return { ok: true };
   257	  const a = String(presented ?? '').split('.');
   258	  const b = current.split('.');
   259	  /** @type {string[]} */
   260	  const drifted = [];

    39	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root']);
    40	const EXIT_ESCALATED = 3;
    41	const EXIT_PARKED = 4;
    42	
    43	/**
    44	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    45	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    46	 */
    47	export async function cmdPipeline(args, io) {
    48	  /** @type {string[]} */
    49	  const positionals = [];
    50	  /** @type {string[]} */
    51	  const flagTokens = [];
    52	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    53	    const a = args[i];
    54	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
    55	      flagTokens.push(a);
    56	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
    57	        i += 1;
    58	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
    59	      }
    60	    } else {
    61	      positionals.push(a);
    62	    }
    63	  }
    64	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, {});
    65	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'pipeline', parsed.error);
    66	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    67	
    68	  const sub = positionals[0];
    69	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton pipeline run');
    70	  if (sub !== 'run' && sub !== 'resume') return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: run, resume)`);
    71	  if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
    72	  if (sub === 'resume') flags.__resume = true;
    73	
    74	  return runPipeline(flags, io);
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * Derive the repo's trunk branch EXACTLY ONCE per run: origin's HEAD when it
    79	 * exists, else the currently checked-out branch at the root. The result is
    80	 * stamped into state and reused on every resume — never re-derived mid-run.
    81	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    82	 * @returns {Promise<string>}
    83	 */
    84	async function deriveTrunk(root, io) {
    85	  try {
    86	    const out = String((await io.execFile('git', ['symbolic-ref', 'refs/remotes/origin/HEAD'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
    87	    const m = out.match(/refs\/remotes\/origin\/(.+)$/);
    88	    if (m?.[1]) return m[1];
    89	  } catch {
    90	    // No origin HEAD (local-only repo) — fall back to the checked-out branch.
    91	  }
    92	  const branch = String((await io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
    93	  return branch.length > 0 && branch !== 'HEAD' ? branch : 'main';
    94	}
    95	
    96	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
    97	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
    98	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
    99	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
   100	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
   101	  }
   102	  const seen = new Set();
   103	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
   104	    const st = subtasks[i];
   105	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
   106	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
   107	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
   108	    seen.add(st.id);
   109	  }
   110	  return null;
   111	}
   112

   125	  return { ok: true, spec: { ...s, constraints, budgets, smoke } };

There is a substantive integration concern: the temp spec uses a role absent from `baton.config.json`, but `validateLoopSpec` currently defines that as a usage error, not a parkable “no eligible assignment” case. I’m checking whether the detach parent could still satisfy the integration by delegating validation to the child, or whether the fixture should use a known role with an unavailable/empty chain instead.

   222	  }
   223	  io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmdLabel}: could not acquire the run lock after reclaiming — another supervisor keeps winning\n`);
   224	  return { ok: false, code: 1 };
   225	}

   227	/**
   228	 * The supervisor state machine: drives the spec's phases through headless
   229	 * role children, records every transition in the journal-replayable loop
   230	 * state, enforces the 5-cap, pauses at the smoke gate, and hands limit
   231	 * deaths to the failover transaction. Children run via the injectable seam
   232	 * `io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild`.
   233	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
   234	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   235	 */
   236	async function runLoop(flags, io) {
   237	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   238	  const specPath = `${root}/loop.json`;
   239	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   240	  const lockPath = `${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`;
   241	
   242	  // Preconditions — before any lock or spawn.
   243	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, specPath);
   244	  if (!rawSpec.ok) {
   245	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no loop.json at ${specPath} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
   246	  }
   247	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   248	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
   249	  const validated = validateLoopSpec(rawSpec.value, config);
   250	  if (validated.ok !== true) {
   251	    return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `loop.json is invalid:\n  - ${validated.errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
   252	  }
   253	  const spec = validated.spec;
   254	
   255	  // Supervisor run lock — atomic exclusive acquisition; provably-dead owners
   256	  // are reclaimed with their recorded child groups reaped first (B8 + G4).
   257	  const lock = await acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, 'loop run');
   258	  if (lock.ok !== true) return lock.code;
   259	
   260	  // Load or initialize the run state — stamped with {flavor, specDigest} so
   261	  // cross-flavor, changed-spec, or pre-stamp resumes refuse instead of
   262	  // misaligning (H5 + I3). These refusals release the lock explicitly — an
   263	  // early return here must not leave supervisor.lock behind (I4).
   264	  const specDigest = dedupeKey(spec.phases);
   265	  let { state } = await loadLoopState(root, io);
   266	  if (state === null) {
   267	    // A resume with nothing persisted is a wrong-directory mistake, not a
   268	    // fresh run — refusing beats silently initializing and running (N2).
   269	    if (flags.__resume === true) {
   270	      lock.release();
   271	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'nothing to resume — no run state exists here; start with: baton loop run');
   272	    }
   273	    state = { ...initLoopState(spec, io), flavor: 'loop', specDigest };
   274	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   275	  } else {
   276	    if (typeof state.flavor === 'string' && state.flavor !== 'loop') {
   277	      lock.release();
   278	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `the persisted run state is flavor '${state.flavor}' — a ${state.flavor} run cannot be resumed as a loop (flavor mismatch); archive .handoff/loop or finish the ${state.flavor} run first`);
   279	    }
   280	    if (typeof state.flavor !== 'string' || typeof state.specDigest !== 'string') {
   281	      lock.release();
   282	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'the persisted run state is missing its {flavor, specDigest} stamp (pre-stamp legacy state) — resuming it could misalign the run; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
   283	    }
   284	    if (state.specDigest !== specDigest) {
   285	      lock.release();
   286	      return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop.json phases changed since this run started (spec digest mismatch) — resuming would misalign the phase position; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');
   287	    }
   288	  }
   289	  const runId = state.runId;
   290	  // The lock payload's runId now correlates with the run it guards (H8).
   291	  io.fs.writeFileSync(`${p.dir}/supervisor.lock`, JSON.stringify({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, runId }));
   292	
   293	  /** Apply one event, journal it, persist the snapshot — one transition. */
   294	  const transition = async (/** @type {any} */ ev) => {
   295	    const next = applyLoopEvent(state, ev);
   296	    const seq = await appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io);
   297	    state = { ...next, journalSeq: seq };
   298	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   299	    return state;
   300	  };
   301	
   302	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   303	  // The doctor's cached availability probes (fresh ≤15 min) skip a
   304	  // rate-limited platform at spawn time (v1.1 item 8); a missing or stale
   305	  // cache reads null — offline-degraded, exactly as before.
   306	  const probes = readProbeCache(root, io);
   307	  const runner = io.superviseChild ?? superviseChild;
   308	  // The runId is already 'loop-<hex>' — no extra prefix (D5).
   309	  const sessionHint = runId;
   310	  /** @type {string[]} */
   311	  let avoid = [];
   312	  /** @type {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} */
   313	  let avoidEntries = [];
   314	  let childSeq = 0;
   315	  // Child spend is budgeted PER PHASE (plan item 3) — a runaway phase parks
   316	  // itself without eating the other phases' budgets.
   317	  /** @type {Map<string, number>} */
   318	  const phaseChildren = new Map();
   319	  // Persisted per-gate findings (plan item 4): the latest non-empty line
   320	  // survives parks, crashes, and re-invocations.
   321	  /** @param {string} gate @returns {string} */
   322	  const latestFindings = (gate) => {
   323	    const path = `${p.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`;
   324	    if (!io.fs.existsSync(path)) return '';
   325	    let last = '';
   326	    for (const line of String(io.fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8')).split('\n')) {
   327	      if (line.trim() === '') continue;
   328	      try {
   329	        const rec = JSON.parse(line);
   330	        if (typeof rec?.findings === 'string' && rec.findings.length > 0) last = rec.findings;
   331	      } catch {
   332	        // A torn line proves nothing — skip it.
   333	      }
   334	    }
   335	    return last;

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/roles/resolve.mjs | sed -n '1,120p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>, nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], avoidEntries = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  // Entry-level avoidance (plan §Model-level failover): a specific
    54	  // {platform, model} tuple is dead (e.g. account-tier rejected) while its
    55	  // platform stays usable. Matching is on model IDENTITY — an @effort suffix
    56	  // on either side is ignored — and requires BOTH dimensions, so the same
    57	  // model name on another platform is never caught.
    58	  /** @param {any} m */
    59	  const baseModel = (m) => (typeof m === 'string' ? m.split('@')[0] : m);
    60	  /** @param {string} platform @param {string} model */
    61	  const entryAvoided = (platform, model) =>
    62	    avoidEntries.some((a) => a?.platform === platform && baseModel(a?.model) === baseModel(model));
    63	
    64	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    65	  const assignments = {};
    66	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    67	    /** @type {any[]} */
    68	    const skipped = [];
    69	    /** @type {any} */
    70	    let assignment = null;
    71	
    72	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    73	      const entry = chain[i];
    74	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    75	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    76	        continue;
    77	      }
    78	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    79	      if (why) {
    80	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    81	        continue;
    82	      }
    83	      // After skipReason so a dual match keeps the platform-level label.
    84	      if (entryAvoided(entry.platform, entry.model)) {
    85	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'avoided-entry' });
    86	        continue;
    87	      }
    88	      assignment = {
    89	        platform: entry.platform,
    90	        model: entry.model,
    91	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    92	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    93	        chainIndex: i,
    94	        skipped,
    95	      };
    96	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    97	      break;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
   101	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
   102	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
   103	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
   104	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
   105	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   106	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
   107	      }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    if (!assignment) {
   111	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   112	    }
   113	    assignments[role] = assignment;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  return { assignments, notes };
   117	}

     8	export function loadConfig(cwd, io) {
     9	  const file = `${cwd}/baton.config.json`;
    10	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    11	  const errors = [];
    12	
    13	  let raw;
    14	  try {
    15	    raw = io.fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    16	  } catch {
    17	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json not found at ${file} — run 'baton init' to scaffold it` }] };
    18	  }
    19	  let parsed;
    20	  try {
    21	    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    22	  } catch (err) {
    23	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json is not valid JSON (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'parse error'})` }] };
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  if (parsed.roles === null || typeof parsed.roles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed.roles)) {
    27	    errors.push({ path: 'roles', msg: 'roles must be an object mapping role names to chain arrays' });
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  /** @type {Record<string, any[]>} */
    31	  const roles = {};
    32	  if (errors.length === 0) {
    33	    for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(parsed.roles)) {
    34	      if (!Array.isArray(chain)) {
    35	        errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}`, msg: 'a role chain must be an array of "platform/model[@effort]" strings' });
    36	        continue;
    37	      }
    38	      /** @type {any[]} */
    39	      const entries = [];
    40	      for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    41	        const s = chain[i];
    42	        const slash = typeof s === 'string' ? s.indexOf('/') : -1;
    43	        if (slash <= 0 || slash === s.length - 1) {
    44	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `malformed chain entry ${JSON.stringify(s)} — expected "platform/model[@effort]"` });
    45	          continue;
    46	        }
    47	        const platform = s.slice(0, slash);
    48	        const rest = s.slice(slash + 1);
    49	        const at = rest.indexOf('@');
    50	        const model = at === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, at);
    51	        const effort = at === -1 ? null : rest.slice(at + 1);
    52	        if (parsed.platforms && !(platform in parsed.platforms)) {
    53	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `unknown platform "${platform}" — not a key of config.platforms` });
    54	          continue;
    55	        }
    56	        entries.push({ platform, model, effort });
    57	      }
    58	      roles[role] = entries;
    59	    }
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  if (errors.length > 0) return { config: null, errors };
    63	  return { config: { ...parsed, roles }, errors };
    64	}

     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     4	import { loadConfig } from '../../core/src/roles/matrix.mjs';
     5	
     6	// ===========================================================================
     7	// Contract choices for roles/matrix.mjs. Source of truth: docs/design/core.md
     8	// §Module APIs (loadConfig(cwd, io) -> {config, errors[]}); plan §Core engine —
     9	// Role matrix; baton.config.json at the repo root (the real example, whose
    10	// roles are ARRAYS of "platform/model[@effort]" chain-entry strings).
    11	//
    12	// PINS:
    13	//   1. loadConfig reads `<cwd>/baton.config.json` via io.fs. It returns
    14	//      {config, errors} — never throws on bad input. `errors` is a
    15	//      {path, msg}[] array (same shape as validateBundle in schema.mjs);
    16	//      empty when the config is fully valid.
    17	//   2. CHAIN-ENTRY PARSING. Each role chain string is parsed into a structured
    18	//      entry: "platform/model"       -> {platform, model, effort: null}
    19	//                "platform/model@eff" -> {platform, model, effort: 'eff'}.
    20	//      Split on the FIRST '/' (platform names contain '-' but never '/'); the
    21	//      remainder splits on '@' into model + effort. Models keep '.'/'-'
    22	//      (e.g. gpt-5.6-sol). config.roles becomes {[role]: Entry[]}.
    23	//   3. PASSTHROUGH. config.schema, config.platforms, config.defaults (and other
    24	//      top-level keys) survive onto the returned config; the resolver consumes
    25	//      platforms + defaults.
    26	//   4. PATHED ERRORS (path strings pinned as a contract):
    27	//        - config file missing entirely  -> config null; an error whose msg
    28	//          names 'baton.config.json'.
    29	//        - unparseable JSON               -> config null; an error naming the file.
    30	//        - missing `roles` key            -> error path 'roles'.
    31	//        - a role chain that is not an array -> error path 'roles.<role>'.
    32	//        - a malformed entry (no '/')     -> error path 'roles.<role>[<i>]'.
    33	//        - an entry whose platform is not a key of config.platforms
    34	//                                          -> error path 'roles.<role>[<i>]',
    35	//                                             msg mentioning the platform.
    36	//   5. FORWARD-COMPAT. Unknown ROLE NAMES (not one of the seven methodology
    37	//      roles) are TOLERATED — no error; the unknown role is still parsed.
    38	// ===========================================================================
    39	
    40	const CWD = '/repo';
    41	const CONFIG_PATH = '/repo/baton.config.json';
    42	
    43	function ioWithConfig(obj) {
    44	  const files = obj === undefined ? {} : { [CONFIG_PATH]: typeof obj === 'string' ? obj : JSON.stringify(obj) };
    45	  return makeIo({ files });
    46	}
    47	
    48	// The real repo config, as an object (roles still in chain-entry STRING form).
    49	function realConfigObject() {

    57	      implementer: ['claude-code/claude-fable-5', 'codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh', 'cursor/composer'],
